We further establish theoretical properties governing ambiguity, camouflage density, semantic preservation, multi-observation leakage, and temporal multiplexing, and present a rendering algorithm with computational complexity and a pre-registered evaluation protocol. MCCT provides a mathematically rigorous foundation for behaviorally adaptive, accessibility-aware, and computationally resilient digital assessment by securing rendered assessment content while preserving readability for legitimate users.

</details>


### [60] [Robust Dempster-Shafer Evidence Fusion with Chaos-Conflict Measurement and Historical-Experience Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13108)
*Huiyu Li,Weibo Liu,Xinru Xu,Dongchen Gao,Meng Zhang,Junhua Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的证据推理框架，通过混沌-冲突度量联合量化跨证据冲突与证据内非特异性，并利用历史经验驱动的加权方案（谱聚类+后悔理论）结合混合组合规则与信念区间决策策略，在16个真实数据集上取得优于八种DST基线和三种梯度提升方法的性能（平均F1 85.78，平均AUC 93.30）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Dempster-Shafer理论下的证据融合面临两大挑战：一是现有冲突度量独立评估证据间不一致性与证据内不确定性，评估不完整；二是当前融合方法仅通过瞬时比较评估证据源，未利用其跨不同决策场景的长期可靠性信息。

Method: 提出统一证据推理框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 混沌-冲突度量，联合量化跨证据冲突与证据内非特异性，并证明了五个正式性质以保证评估一致性；(2) 历史经验驱动的加权方案，通过谱聚类划分决策空间，应用后悔理论从过去融合结果中计算上下文特定的可靠性画像；(3) 混合组合规则，根据全局冲突水平自适应地在不确定性保留与加权共识之间平衡，随后采用信念区间决策策略实现鲁棒分类而不丢弃认知不确定性。

Result: 在16个真实基准数据集上的实验表明，该框架平均F1得分为85.78，平均AUC为93.30，优于八种DST基线方法和三种梯度提升方法。消融分析确认了各提出组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架为多源决策中自适应证据融合提供了一种有效途径，通过联合度量与历史经验驱动机制弥补了现有DST方法的不足。

Abstract: Multi-source evidence fusion under Dempster-Shafer theory faces two persistent challenges: existing conflict measures assess inter-evidence inconsistency and intra-evidence uncertainty independently, yielding incomplete evaluations, and current fusion methods evaluate evidence sources exclusively through instantaneous comparisns without exploiting their long-term reliability across diverse decision contexts. This paper proposes a unified evidence reasoning framework that addresses both limitations. Specifically, a chaos-conflict measurement is introduced to jointly quantify cross-evidence conflict and intra-evidence non-specificity, with five formally proven properties ensuring consistent assessment. A historical experience driven weighting scheme partitions the decision space via spectral clustering and applies regret theory to compute context-specific reliability profiles from past fusion outcomes. These mechanisms feed into a hybrid combination rule that adaptively balances uncertainty preservation against weighted consensus, controlled by the global conflict level, followed by a belief-interval decision strategy that enables robust classification without discarding epistemic uncertainty. Experiments on 16 real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves an average F1 score of 85.78 and a mean AUC of 93.30, outperforming eight DST-based baselines and three gradient boosting methods. Ablation analysis confirms the contribution of each component we proposed. The framework offers an effective approach for adaptive evidence fusion in multi-source decision making.

</details>


### [61] [SkillEvo: Self-Renewing Evolution Gradients from Multi-Turn Interaction Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13120)
*Qianxi Yan,Chunrong Chen,Jiuzhou Zhao,Min Zhang,Yongzhou Xu,Xiaochuan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillEvo是一个面向Agent技能持续演化的框架，通过多轮用户模拟生成可信反馈作为演化梯度，并引入独立治理层主动修复退化，解决现有方法因单轮QA反馈导致演化停滞和退化累积的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent技能要么人工编写，要么单次LLM生成，缺乏从交互失败中改进的闭环。虽然后续工作试图闭环，但仅依赖单轮问答评估反馈，导致第一轮修补后演化梯度衰减、跨轮缺陷不可见、演化停滞。同时，治理机制仅有端到端验证标量门控，只能拒绝退化候选但无法定位和修复结构性问题。

Method: SkillEvo由两个核心组件构成：一是将多轮用户模拟从评估端点重构为反馈生成器，通过追问逐层暴露缺陷，使每轮修订既消费反馈又产生新反馈；二是用一个独立治理层替代被动标量门控，主动修复事实退化与结构膨胀，防止梯度随退化累积而漂移。

Result: 在六类云服务、9个生产级技能和98个技能引用文件上，SkillEvo超越基于自反思的演化方法23.0分，超越基于单轮QA驱动的演化方法15.4分。

Conclusion: 技能的持续演化的关键约束并非编辑能力或迭代次数，而是评估反馈能否持续提供可信的演化梯度；SkillEvo通过多轮反馈生成与可控治理相结合，实现了可持续的技能演化。

Abstract: Agent Skills are today either hand-authored or produced in a single LLM generation pass, and consequently possess no closed loop through which they might improve from the interaction failures they actually cause. Recent work does close this loop, but derives its feedback from single-turn question-answering evaluation. The consequence is a sharp asymmetry: once the first round has patched the gaps that a single exchange can reveal, the evolution gradient decays, the defects that surface only across multiple turns remain invisible, and evolution stalls. Governance in these systems is likewise driven by an end-to-end verification score, a scalar gate that can reject a degraded candidate but can neither localize nor repair its structural cause. We argue that the binding constraint on sustained skill evolution is neither editing capability nor the number of iterations, but whether the evaluation feedback keeps supplying trustworthy evolution gradients. We introduce SkillEvo, in which trustworthy feedback generates the gradient and controllable governance constrains its direction. The first component recasts multi-turn user simulation from an evaluation endpoint into a feedback generator: follow-up questions expose defects layer by layer, so that every round of revision both consumes feedback and produces new feedback. The second replaces the passive rejection of a scalar gate with an independent governance layer that actively repairs factual degradation and structural bloat, preventing the gradient from drifting as degradation accumulates. Across six categories of cloud services, 9 production Skills, and 98 skill-reference files, SkillEvo surpasses self-reflection-based evolution by 23.0 points and single- turn-QA-driven evolution by 15.4 points.

</details>


### [62] [Numeracy in Large Language Models: Fundamental Limitations and Paths to Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13129)
*Aoxin Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于大型语言模型（LLM）基础数值理解能力的综述。作者提出"数值基础框架"（NGF），将其分解为表征基础（RG）和程序基础（PG），并以此组织了对诊断基准、失效模式、结构解释和缓解策略的系统梳理。通过对三个前沿模型家族在多个基准上的联合评估，论文指出架构干预（如数字感知分词、Abacus嵌入）仅对从零训练的模型有效，而对预训练模型更实用的是监督微调、推理脚手架和外部工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在数学推理基准上表现强劲，但在基础数值任务（如数值大小比较、大整数运算、分数、科学计数法）上仍不可靠。这些基础数值理解能力尚未被作为一个独立于高层次数学推理的能力维度进行系统研究，缺乏统一的理论框架和评估体系。

Method: 提出NGF框架将数值能力分为RG（将数字符号映射到数值、量级及等价表征）和PG（按数学定义执行算术运算）。基于该框架系统组织近期诊断基准、失效模式、结构解释（分词、位置编码、嵌入几何、预训练数据分布）及缓解策略，并在Number Cookbook、NumericBench、GSM-Symbolic上对三个前沿模型家族进行原子级、上下文级和推理辅助级数值能力的联合评估。

Result: 架构干预（如数字感知分词、Abacus Embeddings）能提升从零训练的模型，但对预训练系统使用者不可用；对预训练模型而言，监督微调、推理脚手架和外部工具更实际有效。论文提供了一套系统的数值能力评估与缓解策略对比结果。

Conclusion: 基础数值理解应与高层次数学推理区分对待，NGF框架为此提供了统一的组织视角。未来应聚焦于更可靠的数值行为改善路径，包括面向预训练系统的实用缓解策略，并提出了部署建议和进一步研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong results on mathematical reasoning benchmarks yet remain unreliable on elementary numerical tasks, including magnitude comparison, large-integer arithmetic, fractions, and scientific notation. This survey examines basic numerical understanding as a capability distinct from high-level mathematical reasoning. We propose the Numerical Grounding Framework (NGF), which decomposes numeracy into Representational Grounding (RG), mapping numeral forms to value, magnitude, and equivalent representations, and Procedural Grounding (PG), executing arithmetic operations in accordance with their mathematical definitions. Using NGF, we organize recent diagnostic benchmarks, failure modes, structural explanations, and mitigation strategies. We review evidence concerning tokenization, positional encoding, embedding geometry, and pretraining-data distribution. We also apply NGF in a coordinated evaluation of three frontier model families across Number Cookbook, NumericBench, and GSM-Symbolic, comparing atomic, contextual, and reasoning-assisted numeracy. Architectural interventions such as digit-aware tokenization and Abacus Embeddings can improve models trained from scratch but are generally unavailable to users of pretrained systems, for whom supervised fine-tuning, reasoning scaffolds, and external tools are more practical. We conclude with deployment recommendations and research directions for more reliable numerical behavior in foundation models.

</details>


### [63] [Rethinking Normalization Placement for LLMs: Post-Norm under Curriculum Depth Growing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13156)
*Sheng Ren,Yadong Wang,Naiqiang Tan,Jiangang Kong,Jun Fang,Rui Liu,Jun Wang,Kai Chen,Lipeng Liang,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究深度课程学习（curriculum depth growth）下Transformer归一化位置（pre-norm vs post-norm）的选择问题。实验发现：联合训练时两种归一化几乎无差异（验证CE差0.0004），但在课程式深度增长时post-norm比pre-norm优势大一个数量级（差0.0328）。通过多种对照实验（post-joint控制、单块控制、冻结控制）排除计算量、浅层块质量等因素，并借助边界诊断表明post-norm保持残差尺度稳定，而pre-norm存在结构token尺度漂移。结论是归一化位置与训练课程应作为耦合的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Transformer普遍采用pre-norm，因为它有利于全深度模型的联合优化。但作者质疑：当深度通过课程学习（curriculum）逐步引入时，这一偏好是否仍然成立。因为在课程式深度增长中，每个新追加的块接收的是已训练前缀产生的边界表示，归一化位置会直接影响到前向条件化（forward conditioning），因此值得探究归一化放置方式与训练课程之间的交互作用。

Method: 采用受控蒸馏实验：以Qwen3-8B为教师，训练一个九层学生模型。对比pre-norm与post-norm在两种训练设置下的表现：(1) 联合训练（joint）；(2) 课程式深度增长（curriculum growth）。并用post-joint控制（按学生活跃层token匹配计算量）、单块控制、冻结控制排除混淆因素，同时进行边界诊断（残差尺度、结构token尺度漂移、identity映射程度）以解释机制。

Result: 联合训练下pre-norm与post-norm几乎无差异（验证CE差0.0004），但课程增长下post-norm优于pre-norm达0.0328，优势大一个数量级。排名在课程过程中发生交叉：追加块后post-norm开始领先。post-joint控制（匹配计算量）仍差于post-grow，排除计算量作为唯一解释。单块与冻结控制将排名变化定位到块追加本身。边界诊断显示post-norm残差尺度稳定，pre-norm存在结构token尺度漂移，且pre-norm最后一个预增长块近似identity映射。

Conclusion: 归一化放置方式与训练课程在蒸馏场景下应被视为耦合的设计选择。课程深度增长改变了pre-norm与post-norm的相对优劣，post-norm在追加块后展现优势，其机制与边界尺度条件化（boundary-scale conditioning）一致。

Abstract: Pre-norm is the standard normalization placement in modern Transformers because it facilitates joint optimization of full-depth models. We ask whether this preference persists when depth is introduced through a curriculum. In curriculum depth growth, each appended block receives the boundary representation produced by a trained prefix, making normalization placement relevant to forward conditioning. We therefore test whether placement and training curriculum interact. In a controlled distillation study with a Qwen3-8B teacher and a nine-layer student, pre-norm and post-norm are indistinguishable under joint training, differing by $0.0004$ validation CE, while post-norm improves over pre-norm by $0.0328$ under curriculum growth, an order of magnitude larger. A post-joint control matched by student active-layer tokens remains worse than post-grow, which rules out compute as the sole explanation. The ranking crosses over during the curriculum: post-norm takes the lead once blocks are appended. Single-block and freeze controls localize the ranking change to block appending rather than shallow-block quality or retraining. Boundary diagnostics associate post-norm with stable residual scales and pre-norm with structural-token scale drift; on a fixed batch, the final pre-grow block is also nearly identity-mapped. Together with the phase-wise crossover, these observations are consistent with boundary-scale conditioning after new blocks are appended. The results motivate treating normalization placement and training curriculum as coupled design choices in this distillation setting.

</details>


### [64] [SkillShapley: Boundary-Adaptive Shapley Valuation for Skill Step Attribution in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13173)
*Chang Liu,Yuqi Zhang,Yiman Zhong,Boyi Liu,Hengjun Wang,Shuyue Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillShapley是一个基于Shapley值的智能体技能步骤级归因框架，用于量化智能体技能（agent skills）中每个步骤对整体任务性能的贡献。该方法分为两阶段：先识别信息丰富的联盟区域，再自适应采样可产生可复用边际证据的新联盟。实验表明其能高效识别高价值或低价值技能步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体技能的创建主要依赖人工手动编写或智能体执行轨迹，缺乏对技能中每个步骤如何影响整体性能的理解。也就是说，如何量化智能体技能中单个步骤的贡献仍是一个开放问题，这阻碍了对技能质量的系统性评估和优化。

Method: 首先将技能步骤归因建模为基于Shapley值的贡献估计问题。在此基础上提出SkillShapley框架，其设计受两个关键实证洞察驱动：(1) 离散化基准奖励导致陡峭的性能悬崖；(2) 步骤间的交互主要是加性的而非协同性的。框架分两个阶段运行：第一阶段识别信息丰富的联盟区域；第二阶段自适应采样能够产生可复用边际证据的新联盟。

Result: 在广泛采用的SkillsBench基准上进行的实验表明，SkillShapley能够有效且高效地识别高价值或低价值的技能步骤，为智能体技能的创建提供了若干关键启示。

Conclusion: SkillShapley成功解决了智能体技能中步骤贡献量化这一开放问题，证明了Shapley值方法在技能步骤归因上的可行性，并为实际智能体技能创建提供了可操作的洞察。

Abstract: Agent skills are crucial external instructions that enable language agents to execute long procedural tasks such as coding or document processing. Existing agent skills are primarily created through human manual crafting or agent execution traces, with limited understanding of how each step contributes to overall skill performance on specific tasks; i.e., there remains an open problem in quantifying the contribution of individual steps within an agent skill. To address this issue, we first model skill-step attribution as a Shapley value-based contribution estimation problem, and then propose SkillShapley, a step-level attribution framework for agent skills. Notably, SkillShapley operates in two phases, motivated by key empirical insights, i.e., discretized benchmark rewards that create sharp performance cliffs, and step interactions that are largely additive rather than synergistic. Specifically, it first identifies informative coalitional regions, and then adaptively samples new coalitions that can yield reusable marginal evidence. Experiments on skills from the widely adopted SkillsBench demonstrate that our SkillShapley can effectively and efficiently identify high- or low-value skill steps, providing several key takeaways for agent skill creation.

</details>


### [65] [Teach the Magnitude, Not the Direction: Verifier-Bounded Credit Assignment for Multi-Turn Multi-step LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13179)
*Zechuan Wang,Siyuan Lu,Hongxuan Zhang,Linjian Mo,Chenyi Zhuang,Leilei Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CrEST，一个分层信用分配框架，结合强化学习的验证器有界上限与密集的逐token监督信号，通过回合级分段验证优势和熵门控自教师调制解决信用分配问题，在BFCL V3和WildToolBench上超越RL和蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）在多轮工具使用智能体训练中存在轨迹级信用分配问题，将异质的每轮（turn）结果混合为单一奖励信号；而在线提炼虽然提供密集逐token监督，但要么受限于教师模型上限，要么易发生梯度集中坍塌。需要一个既能保留RL的验证器有界上限、又能纳入密集token级信号的框架。

Method: CrEST在两层级别解决信用分配：1）回合级（turn-segmented）验证优势，解决回合间稀释（inter-turn dilution）；2）熵门控自教师调制（entropy-gated self-teacher modulation），细化回合内token贡献。核心思想是让教师从决定更新方向降级为调节更新幅度，从而在不牺牲验证器有界上限的前提下实现密集信用分配。

Result: 在BFCL V3和WildToolBench两个基准上，跨越两个模型尺度，CrEST一致性地优于RL和提炼基线。在长轨迹和严格会话级指标上取得最大提升。

Conclusion: 证明了在策略优化中，教师角色可以从事关更新方向的决定者转变为更新幅度的调节者，从而在不牺牲验证器有界上限的情况下解锁密集信用分配。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) offers a verifier-bounded performance ceiling for training multi-turn tool-use agents, yet its trajectory-level credit assignment conflates heterogeneous per-turn outcomes into a single reward signal. On-policy distillation provides dense per-token supervision but is either teacher-bounded or prone to gradient concentration collapse. We introduce $\textbf{CrEST}$, a hierarchical credit assignment framework that retains RL's verifier-bounded ceiling while incorporating dense token-level signals from a privileged self-teacher. $\textbf{CrEST}$ resolves credit at two levels: turn-segmented verified advantages address inter-turn dilution, while entropy-gated self-teacher modulation refines intra-turn token contributions. Experiments on BFCL V3 and WildToolBench show that $\textbf{CrEST}$ consistently outperforms both RL and distillation baselines across two model scales, with the largest gains on long-trajectory and strict session-level metrics. Our work demonstrates that the teacher's role in policy optimization can be reduced from determining update directions to modulating update magnitudes, unlocking dense credit assignment without sacrificing the verifier-bounded ceiling.

</details>


### [66] [A Unifying Perspective on Causal World Models: From Observations to Representations to Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13456)
*Avinash Kori,Fabrizio Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从因果视角系统性地研究了世界模型（World Models），提出了因果世界模型（Causal World Models, CWMs）的形式化定义，并连接了因果表示学习、对象中心学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策制定等既有工作，同时从可辨识性角度阐明了世界模型的各组件何时能从数据中恢复以及恢复的等价程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型被视作智能体在分布外进行预测、规划和行动的基础，但现有世界模型主要聚焦于生成能力，缺乏对系统动态背后的因果结构的建模与理解。作者认为有用的世界模型需要超越生成能力，捕获实体属性、实体间交互以及实体与环境间交互，以支持因果推理和明智决策。

Method: 本文采用理论分析的方法，从多个抽象层次（从感知观测到环境动态结构的概念表示）研究世界模型。作者提出了因果世界模型的形式化定义，将其与因果表示学习、对象中心学习、因果发现、结构因果模型及基于模型的决策制定等已有工作进行衔接，并从可辨识性角度分析世界模型的各组件在何种条件下可从数据中恢复及恢复的等价性。

Result: 论文提供了一种基于任务驱动的因果世界模型（CWMs）形式化框架，建立了世界建模与因果表示学习等领域之间的联系，并阐明了世界模型组件的可辨识性条件及其等价关系。

Conclusion: 本文通过将世界模型锚定在支持因果推理和知情决策的表示与结构上，为世界模型的研究提供了因果视角的理论基础，有助于推动世界模型从纯生成式模型向支持因果推理与决策的智能体基础模型发展。

Abstract: World Models (WM) are increasingly seen as a foundation for intelligent agents that can predict, plan, and act beyond their training distribution. In this paper, we study WMs from a causal perspective across multiple levels of abstraction, ranging from perceptual observations to building a conceptual representation of the structure governing the environment dynamics. We argue that useful WMs must go beyond generative capabilities alone: they should also capture entity properties, entity-to-entity interactions, and entity-to-environment interactions that determine and explain the dynamics of a system. We provide a formal definition of Causal WMs (CWMs) grounded in the tasks they are intended to support, connecting world modelling with existing work in causal representation learning, object-centric learning, causal discovery, structural causal models, and model-based decision-making. Finally, we relate CWMs to the literature on identifiability, clarifying when the components of a WM can be recovered from data and up to which equivalence. With this, we ground WMs in representations and structures that support causal reasoning and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [67] [TsuGO: Probing Search Efficiency in LLM Reasoning via Go Life-and-Death Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13221)
*Shunwen Bai,Ziping Ma,Chaoyang Zhang,Yarong Wang,Jiale Liu,Zhen Qin,Qingpei Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TsuGO，一个基于围棋生死问题的过程级推理基准，用于评估LLM在推理中的搜索效率，通过将思维链解析为结构化搜索树，将搜索组织的评估与领域知识解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理评估从最终答案准确性转向过程级评估，但现有方法仅关注思维链的连贯性和冗余度，未能捕捉模型如何规划推理路径和分配推理资源（即如何组织搜索）。大多数基准任务具有单一目标且可通过静态能力（如推演和工具使用）解决，导致搜索组织这一维度未被测量。

Method: TsuGO利用围棋死活问题构建一个具有封闭且可验证解空间、并具有内在对抗性结构的基准。通过约束解空间来解耦领域知识与搜索组织，将思维链解析为结构化搜索树，并报告搜索效率（Search Efficiency）、Token效率（Token Efficiency）及诊断指标和可视化结果。

Result: 实验表明：当前LLM在围棋死活问题解决上远不稳定；较强的模型通过更早找到正确候选并在有效分支上持续投入努力而成功；但大多数模型的行为更接近无引导的搜索算法，而非神经引导的KataGo。更长的思维链或更高的Token效率不一定意味着更好的搜索表现。

Conclusion: 搜索组织和推理资源分配是LLM推理评估中缺失的关键维度，TsuGO为这一维度提供了可量化的评测框架。

Abstract: The evaluation of LLM reasoning is moving from final-answer accuracy to process-level assessment, yet existing methods still fail to capture how models plan reasoning paths and allocate reasoning resources--that is, how they organize search. Prior process-level methods focus on the coherence and redundancy of chain-of-thought (CoT), and most benchmark tasks have a single objective solvable by static capabilities such as derivation and tool use, leaving search organization unmeasured. We introduce TsuGO, a process-level reasoning benchmark for evaluating Search Efficiency in LLM reasoning through Go life-and-death problems. These problems provide closed and verifiable solution spaces with an inherent adversarial structure, making candidate generation, response checking, branch comparison, and backtracking necessary parts of reasoning rather than incidental trace patterns. By constraining the solution space, TsuGO disentangles domain knowledge from search organization, parses CoT into a structured search tree, and reports Search Efficiency together with Token Efficiency and other diagnostic metrics and visualizations. Experiments show that current LLMs remain far from stable tsumego solving: stronger models succeed by finding the correct candidate earlier and sustaining effort on productive branches, but most models still behave much closer to unguided search algorithms than to neural-guided KataGo. Longer CoT or higher Token Efficiency does not necessarily imply better search. Our results identify search organization and reasoning-resource allocation as missing dimensions in LLM reasoning evaluation.

</details>


### [68] [Capability Sheaves for Compositional Agent-Harness Repair: Controlled Quotients and a Real-Repository Stress Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13228)
*Saveliy Batruin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"能力层"(capability sheaf)框架来建模多智能体系统中各组件对共享状态不一致导致的失败，用有限CSP定义接受性，用相对上同调类作为诊断工具。受控实验证明了该方法的无关变量不变性（可通过商化将候选预算从2000降至1000），但在SWE-bench Multilingual/PatchFuseBench真实数据上，共轭方法与非共轭基线无显著差异（118 vs 116，p=0.75），留一仓库外推门控达到127/160但与强基线持平，真实世界的共轭优越性未得到支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（agent harness）集成了检索、路由、状态、溯源和验证等组件，但局部成功的组件可能对共享状态产生分歧导致失败。作者希望用严格的数学框架（层论/上同调）来建模这种不一致性，并提供可计算的诊断与搜索方法。

Method: (1) 用有限能力层建模系统：stalk编码类型化行为签名，限制映射保留共享字段；(2) 将接受性定义为有限CSP；(3) 用线性化相对上同调类作为诊断与搜索特征；(4) 受控实验中引入隐藏中间中介者的原始状态作为无关变量，通过商化coboundaries降低候选预算；(5) 在SWE-bench Multilingual的PatchFuseBench发现集上进行真实评测，包括池级构造、候选级修复以及留一仓库外推门控。

Result: 受控实验：商化coboundaries将候选预算从2,000降至1,000，精确对齐隐藏状态消除差距，精确CSP与商化结果匹配，证明的是对陈旧代表的不变性而非优于精确推理。真实评测：池级构造因[b-Dx]=[b]在coker D中恒定而无法排序；候选级修复在848/875个候选上非平凡，120/160个issue内有变化；解决118个issue vs 基线116个，但跨仓库差异不显著(p=0.75)；留一仓库门控达到127/160，与强锚点持平(p=1.0)。确认集保持密封。

Conclusion: 研究支持受控实验中的不变性机制和可识别性修正，但不支持真实世界中上同调方法具备实际优势。作者诚实地报告了负面结果，强调发现门控失败而确认集未打开。

Abstract: Agent harnesses combine retrieval, routing, state, provenance, and verification, but locally successful components may disagree on shared state. We model this failure with a finite \emph{capability sheaf}: stalks encode typed behavior signatures, restriction maps retain shared fields, and accepted runs are useful global sections. An exact finite constraint-satisfaction problem (CSP) defines acceptance, while a linearized relative cohomology class provides a diagnostic and search feature.
  A controlled experiment over 20 task clusters introduces hidden interior mediators whose raw states are nuisance variables. Quotienting their coboundaries reduces the candidate budget from 2,000 to 1,000 per cluster; aligning the hidden state removes the gap. Exact CSP matches the quotient, so the result demonstrates invariance to stale representatives, not superiority over exact reasoning.
  We then test the method on a discovery split from the SWE-bench Multilingual pool of PatchFuseBench: 160 issues from 20 repositories, 875 real candidate patches, 2,579 source-aware edit atoms, and 153 newly executed patches. A first pool-level construction is constant because $[b-Dx]=[b]$ in $\operatorname{coker}D$ and therefore cannot rank configurations. A candidate-indexed repair is nontrivial on 848/875 candidates and varies within 120/160 issues. It resolves 118 issues versus 116 for a matched noncohomological selector, but the difference is not supported across repositories (exact sign-flip $p=0.75$). A leave-one-repository-out abstention gate reaches 127/160, tying the strong anchor and exceeding its matched gate by one issue ($p=1.0$). The discovery gate therefore fails and the confirmatory split remains sealed. The study supports the controlled invariance mechanism and an identifiability correction, but not a real-world cohomological advantage.

</details>


### [69] [vToken: Token-Level Virtualization for Reclaimable KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13263)
*Yuanhang Gao,Xiangrui Yang,Yuanfeng Chen,Hongjia Chen,Qianru Lv,Wenfei Wu,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: vToken是一个轻量级的token级虚拟化层，通过token表间接寻址和异步实时token重打包机制，解耦逻辑token活跃度与物理块放置，从而解决KV缓存驱逐算法与PagedAttention块级管理之间的不匹配导致的块内碎片化问题。在vLLM中实现后，相比Naive-Evict基线减少了27.2%-72.3%的保留KV块，SLA约束吞吐量提升最高1.37倍，最大可行并发度提升2倍，同时集成代码量从500+行降至50行以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理服务面临KV缓存内存瓶颈。PagedAttention使用固定大小内存块来减少分配器级碎片，但现有的KV驱逐算法以token粒度操作，比块级管理更细，这种粒度不匹配导致块内碎片化（intra-block fragmentation），使大量已分配的KV内存无法被回收利用。

Method: vToken采用token级虚拟化层设计：通过token-table间接寻址维护稳定的逻辑token视图，将逻辑token活跃度与物理块放置解耦；通过异步重打包（repacking）活跃token实现物理内存的回收；设计上保持PagedAttention内核和CUDA Graph兼容性。

Result: 在vLLM中使用H2O、Random和Scissorhands等多种驱逐策略和多种模型进行评估。相比Naive-Evict基线，vToken减少每请求保留KV块27.2%-72.3%，SLA约束吞吐量提升最多1.37倍；在受限active-KV预算下，最大可行并发度扩展最多2倍；每种驱逐策略的集成代码量从500+行减少到50行以内。

Conclusion: vToken通过token级虚拟化层有效解决了KV驱逐粒度与块级内存管理之间的不匹配问题，显著降低了内存碎片化，提升了KV缓存的利用率和系统吞吐量，同时大幅简化了不同驱逐策略的集成复杂度，且保持了与现有PagedAttention内核和CUDA Graph的兼容性。

Abstract: Large language model serving faces a critical memory bottleneck: the KV cache grows with sequence length and batch size. PagedAttention uses fixed-size memory blocks to reduce allocator-level fragmentation, but recent KV eviction algorithms operate at a token granularity finer than block-level management. This mismatch causes intra-block fragmentation, leaving a large fraction of allocated KV memory unreclaimable. We present vToken, a lightweight token-level virtualization layer that decouples logical token liveness from physical block placement. vToken maintains a stable logical token view through token-table indirection and realizes physical reclamation by repacking live tokens asynchronously. The design preserves PagedAttention kernels and CUDA Graph compatibility. We implement vToken in vLLM and evaluate it with H2O, Random, and Scissorhands across models. Compared with a paired Naive-Evict baseline, vToken reduces retained KV blocks per request by 27.2\%--72.3\% and improves SLA-constrained throughput by up to 1.37$\times$. Under a constrained active-KV budget, it extends the maximum feasible concurrency by up to 2$\times$, while reducing the per-policy integration footprint from 500+ lines to under 50.

</details>


### [70] [Towards Context-Aware Clinical Motion Understanding in Daily Living at Home: Freezing of Gait Detection with Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13283)
*Vayalet Stefanova,Diwas Lamsal,Margot Genbrugge,Maxim Yudayev,Christian Schlenstedt,Moran Gilat,Bart Vanrumste,Benjamin Filtjens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究日常生活活动中帕金森病患者的冻结步态（FOG）检测，探索自我中心视觉能否为可穿戴IMU传感器补充任务相关的上下文信息。在13名帕金森患者的家庭环境中，同步采集自我中心视频、IMU数据和专家标注的FOG标签，比较预训练的自我中心视频基础模型、时间序列基础模型以及从头训练的IMU-TCN模型性能。结果表明IMU-TCN表现最佳（F1 42.3, AUROC 83.0），优于V-JEPA2自我中心视频特征（F1 32.6, AUROC 77.2），但自我中心视频仍显示高于随机水平的判别能力，表明其可能捕获与IMU互补的FOG相关信息，支持将预训练的自我中心视频表征用于增强基于可穿戴传感器的临床运动理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常生活活动中的运动理解需要超越运动学信息的上下文，因为相似的运动模式可能对应有意停止、物体交互或病理性运动障碍。冻结步态（FOG）作为帕金森病在ADL中受情境因素强烈影响的症状，为研究自我中心视觉是否能提供有助于区分这些情况的上下文信息提供了理想的探索场景。

Method: 研究从13名帕金森患者的家庭环境中收集同步的自我中心视频、可穿戴IMU数据和专家标注的FOG标签。采用留一被试出（leave-one-subject-out）评估方案，比较三组模型：1）预训练的自我中心视频基础模型（如V-JEPA2）的冻结表征；2）时间序列基础模型的冻结表征；3）从头训练的IMU-based TCN模型。通过事件检测F1分数和AUROC进行性能评估，并进行定性分析。

Result: IMU-based TCN取得了最强的事件检测性能（F1 42.3, AUROC 83.0），明显优于V-JEPA2自我中心视频特征（F1 32.6, AUROC 77.2）。虽然自我中心视频单独未超过IMU感知方法，但其表现高于随机水平，且定性分析表明自我中心视觉可能捕获与IMU相互独立的FOG相关信息。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持在基于可穿戴传感器的临床运动理解中引入预训练的自我中心视频表征以补充上下文信息。尽管当前IMU仍是更强的单一模态，自我中心视觉展现出的互补潜力值得进一步探索，尤其是在日常生活中的FOG检测等情境敏感任务中。

Abstract: Understanding motion in daily living requires context beyond kinematics, because similar inertial patterns during activities of daily living (ADLs) can reflect intentional stopping, object interaction, or pathological movement impairment. Egocentric vision provides task-related context that may help disambiguate these cases. We investigate this challenge through freezing of gait (FOG) detection in Parkinson's disease (PD), a symptom strongly influenced by contextual factors during ADLs. Using synchronized egocentric video, wearable IMUs, and expert-annotated FOG labels collected from 13 PD participants in their homes, we evaluate frozen representations from pretrained ego-video and time-series foundation models, alongside an IMU-based TCN trained from scratch, under leave-one-subject-out evaluation. The IMU-based TCN achieved the strongest event-detection performance, reaching 42.3 F1 and 83.0 AUROC, compared with 32.6 F1 and 77.2 AUROC for V-JEPA2 ego-video features. Although ego-video alone did not outperform IMU-based sensing, it showed above-chance discrimination, and qualitative analyses suggest that egocentric vision may capture FOG-relevant information independent of IMUs. Together, these results support the use of pretrained ego-video representations to add contextual information to wearable-sensor-based clinical motion understanding in daily living.

</details>


### [71] [NAS-Driven Hardware Accelerator Exploration for Edge AI and Quantization Effects on the Pareto Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13293)
*Eleftherios Mylonas,Angelos Kouprizas,Michael Birbas,Alexios Birbas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套三阶段流水线（硬件无关的Pareto排序代理前端、带Pareto感知过滤与反馈控制的量化桥接模块、基于CGRA4ML的进化式域空间探索后端），并首次实证研究了INT4后训练量化（PTQ）如何扰动NAS-Bench-201上的Pareto空间，发现FP32零样本代理在Pareto空间覆盖率上优于专门的INT4训练代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI部署需要同时满足精度、计算效率和硬件可部署性的神经架构。虽然近期工作将量化直接纳入NAS搜索循环，但这增加了搜索复杂度并紧耦合了架构与量化设计。而更简单的“搜索后量化”策略缺乏系统性分析：PTQ对NAS发现的Pareto结构的影响尚未被刻画，也缺乏将量化架构映射到可重构加速器并自动探索硬件的统一框架。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 在NAS-Bench-201上训练硬件无关的Pareto等级代理前端；(2) 量化桥接模块，包含Pareto感知过滤与反馈控制；(3) 在CGRA4ML平台上采用进化式域空间探索（DSE）后端寻找最优硬件映射。同时，对所有15,625个架构基于真实数据利用形式化稳定性指标，实证研究INT4 PTQ如何扰动NAS-Bench-201的Pareto空间。

Result: 实证结果表明，FP32零样本代理在Pareto空间覆盖率上优于专门的INT4训练代理（在两种标准搜索策略下均成立）。研究首次刻画了INT4 PTQ对NAS-Bench-201全部15,625个架构Pareto空间的扰动特性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了PTQ对NAS Pareto结构影响缺乏分析、以及缺少统一量化架构到可重构加速器映射框架的两大空白，表明后搜索量化策略配合适当的代理选择即可实现高效可靠的硬件部署。

Abstract: Edge AI deployment demands neural architectures that are simultaneously accurate, computationally efficient, and hardware-deployable - a challenge addressed by hardware-aware Neural Architecture Search (NAS). While recent works incorporate quantization directly into the NAS loop, these approaches expand search complexity and tightly couple architecture and quantization design. The simpler post-search quantization strategy has received little analytical attention: the effects of Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) on the NAS-discovered Pareto structure remain uncharacterised, and no framework combines quantized architecture mapping onto reconfigurable accelerators with automated hardware exploration. This paper addresses both gaps. First, a three-stage pipeline is proposed: a hardware-agnostic Pareto rank surrogate frontend on NAS-Bench-201, a quantization bridge with Pareto-aware filtering and feedback control, and an evolutionary Domain Space Exploration (DSE) backend on CGRA4ML for optimal hardware mapping. Second, an empirical study characterises how INT4 PTQ perturbs the NAS-Bench-201 Pareto space through formal stability metrics on ground-truth data for all 15,625 architectures, and demonstrates that an FP32 zero-shot surrogate outperforms a dedicated INT4-trained surrogate in Pareto space coverage across two standard search strategies.

</details>


### [72] [StateBridge: Training-free Hidden-state Alignment for Latent Communication in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13317)
*Yanwen Peng,Delvin Ce Zhang,Xi Wang,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StateBridge是一种无需训练的潜在通信方法，通过闭式正交变换将发送者的最终隐藏状态对齐到接收者的输入空间，作为连续前缀注入接收者，在数学推理、代码生成和问答任务上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统通常使用离散文本令牌通信，但文本引入了离散瓶颈，将连续隐藏状态转换为离散令牌会丢失信息。虽然已有潜在通信方法（直接传输隐藏表示）被提出，但现有方法要么逐层注入工作记忆（侵入性强），要么需要训练投影器（限制可移植性），缺乏轻量且无需训练的解决方案。

Method: StateBridge通过闭式正交变换将发送者最终层的隐藏状态对齐到接收者的输入空间，结合轻量级范数校准和词汇锚定来确保与预训练输入分布的兼容性，对齐后的状态作为连续前缀拼接到接收者的输入中。整个方法无需训练，具有即插即用的可移植性。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和问答三个任务上使用两个模型家族的四种模型进行评估，StateBridge在26个模型-任务组合中有22个达到最佳或并列最佳分数，持续优于最强基线。

Conclusion: StateBridge证明了无需训练的闭式正交变换即可实现有效的潜在通信，避免了文本瓶颈的信息损失，同时克服了现有潜在通信方法需要训练或侵入式注入的局限，具有良好的可移植性和通用性。

Abstract: Large language model based multi-agent systems usually communicate in text, i.e., using discrete tokens. However, text introduces a discrete bottleneck. Converting the sender's continuous hidden states into discrete tokens discards information that token identities alone cannot capture. Recent work proposes latent communication as an alternative, where agents transmit hidden representations directly without converting them to text. However, existing latent methods either inject working memory layer by layer across the transformers, or require trained projectors that limit portability. We propose StateBridge, a training-free latent communication approach that aligns the sender's final-layer hidden states to the receiver's input space via a closed-form orthogonal transformation. Lightweight norm calibration and vocabulary anchoring ensure compatibility with the pretrained input distribution. The aligned states are prepended to the input of the receiver agent as a continuous prefix. We evaluate StateBridge on math reasoning, code generation, and question answering with four models from two families. StateBridge achieves the best or tied-best score on 22 out of 26 model-task pairs, consistently outperforming the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [73] [LLM-Guided Graph Generation for Structure-Based Local Improvement Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13333)
*Hai Xia,Vaidyanathan Peruvemba Ramaswamy,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个问题无关的自动流水线（SLIM），通过Prompt LLM生成图生成器，将MiniZinc格式的任意约束优化问题实例映射为统一加权图，并利用图结构特征进行变量选择和算法配置选择。在20个MiniZinc竞赛问题上的实验表明，算法选择相对单次Gurobi基线达到39.5%的平均问题加权胜率，超过最佳单一配置（19.3%）的两倍，配置和特征消融后进一步提升至44.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大邻域搜索（LNS）通常随机选择决策变量子集进行迭代优化，不同问题需要针对领域结构特征设计变量选择策略。为了跨问题高效求解，需要一个能够自动提取结构信息并用于变量选择的通用方案。

Method: 构建一个问题无关的自动流水线：(1) 通过LLM并结合语义指南生成图生成器，将任意MiniZinc格式的问题实例映射为统一加权图（节点代表决策变量，边代表约束关系）；(2) 基于此类问题无关图，SLIM框架在变量选择中使用结构信息；(3) 从统一加权图中提取共同的图特征，用于配置选择（algorithm selection）。

Result: 在20个MiniZinc竞赛问题上评估：算法选择达到39.5%的平均问题加权胜率（对比单次Gurobi基线），是最佳单一配置（19.3%）的两倍以上；加入配置和特征消融后进一步提升至44.0%。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义生成能够实现有效的自动结构提取和特征提取，为约束优化问题提供通用且高效的结构化求解方法。

Abstract: Large neighborhood search normally selects a random subset of decision variables for iterative optimization. For efficiently solving different problems, researchers tend to design variable selection strategies by taking into account structural features from different domains. In this paper, we build an automatic pipeline that is problem-agnostic to all problems in the MiniZinc format. By prompting an LLM with our semantic guidelines, we guide the LLM to produce a graph generator that maps any instance of a problem type to a uniform weighted graph, where nodes represent decision variables and edges represent constraint relationships. These problem-agnostic graphs guide our structure-based local improvement framework (SLIM) in variable selection. Meanwhile, the weighted graph enables all problem instances to share the same generic graph representation, from which the same graph features can be extracted and used for configuration selection. We evaluated our pipeline on instances across 20 MiniZinc competition problems, finding that algorithm selection achieves a 39.5% average problem-weighted win rate against a one-shot Gurobi baseline, more than doubling the best single configuration (19.3%). Configuration and feature ablation boost the performance further to 44.0%, demonstrating that LLM-based semantic generation enables effective automated structure extraction and feature extraction for constraint optimization.

</details>


### [74] [LongEarth-R1: Benchmarking and Aligning Vision-Language Models for Long-Horizon Earth Observation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13344)
*Yupan Ding,Jing Xiao,Zhenyuan Zhang,Chaofeng Chen,Liang Liao,Gui-Song Xia,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LongEarth-Bench基准，包含约12万个QA样本（源自11.7万张唯一图像），序列平均15.14帧、最长30帧，覆盖12个长时序地球观测推理任务。并开发LongEarth模型（带序列标识符和结构化思维链的监督微调）及LongEarth-R1（结合格式、时间、空间奖励的GRPO强化学习方法），后者在所有12个长序列任务上取得最优结果，同时在标准遥感基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感视觉语言模型主要关注孤立图像、图像对或短序列，缺乏对长时序地理演化、空间变化定位、时间异常检测和未来推理的可靠框架支撑能力，无法满足长跨度地球观测推理的需求。

Method: (1) 构建LongEarth-Bench基准：约120k QA样本、117k唯一图像、序列平均15.14帧（最长30帧），覆盖12个任务（演化总结、空间推理、异常识别、逻辑预测），并含30k样本的子集提供连接关键帧和变化区域的结构化推理轨迹。(2) 提出LongEarth：通过带显式序列标识符和结构化思维链监督的监督微调训练。(3) 提出LongEarth-R1：在LongEarth基础上应用组相对策略优化（GRPO），结合格式奖励、时间奖励和空间奖励。

Result: LongEarth-R1在全部12个长序列任务上取得最佳结果，同时在标准遥感基准上保持有竞争力的性能，验证了长时序推理能力和通用遥感能力的兼顾。

Conclusion: 通过大规模长序列基准和结合时间/空间奖励的强化学习训练，可以有效提升遥感视觉语言模型的长时序地理演化推理与接地能力，且不强加额外的效果损失。

Abstract: Long-horizon Earth observation reasoning requires models to organize multi-stage geographic evolution, localize spatial changes, detect temporal anomalies, and infer future from extended image sequences. However, existing remote sensing vision-language models mainly focus on isolated images, image pairs, or short sequences, limiting reliable grounding in the relevant frames and regions. We introduce LongEarth-Bench, a benchmark containing approximately 120k question-answering samples derived from 117k unique images. Its sequences average 15.14 frames and extend to 30 frames, covering 12 tasks across evolution summarization, spatial reasoning, anomaly identification, and logical prediction. A 30k-sample subset further provides structured reasoning traces linking key frames and changed regions to final answers. We develop LongEarth through supervised fine-tuning with explicit sequence identifiers and structured chain-of-thought supervision. Building on LongEarth, LongEarth-R1 applies group relative policy optimization with format, temporal, and spatial rewards. LongEarth-R1 achieves the best results on all 12 long-sequence tasks while remaining competitive on standard remote sensing benchmarks.

</details>


### [75] [Rules or Character? Scaling Laws for AI Safety Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13345)
*Satoshi Takahashi,Nobuji Kouno,Masaaki Komatsu,Ryuji Hamamoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个简约的比较静态模型，用于分析AI安全系统中"性格塑造"（如RLHF、宪法AI）与"规则强制"（如输出过滤器）之间的最优资源分配，并发现该最优分配随部署规模扩大的变化方向取决于性格塑造的脆弱性参数而非部署规模本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全系统同时采用训练时的性格塑造和推理时的规则强制两种方法，但缺乏对两者最优平衡如何随部署规模扩大而变化的正式分析。作者旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 构建一个参数化安全设计为资源分配alpha∈[0,1]的比较静态模型，纳入规模相关的过滤器退化、共模故障和性格脆弱性；在乘法Pareto损伤模型下推导期望损害的闭式解，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟补充尾部风险（CVaR）分析。

Result: 在三种场景（乐观、中等、悲观）下，最优alpha*为内点解或处于纯规则边界，并随部署规模T增大而弱向性格塑造方向偏移（Delta alpha*从+0.01到+0.21）。主导参数是基线性格脆弱率，其效应（0.50）远超尾部严重度、过滤器质量或共模故障概率。在较大T时CVaR与期望损害最优值收敛。

Conclusion: 安全架构决策更依赖于性格塑造在分布偏移下的可靠性，而非部署规模本身。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) safety systems combine character shaping (e.g., Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback [RLHF], Constitutional AI), which modifies behavioral distributions at training time, with rule enforcement (e.g., output filters, safety classifiers), which blocks harmful outputs at inference time, yet little formal analysis exists on how their optimal balance should change as deployment scales increase. We introduce a stylized comparative-statics model that parameterizes safety design as a resource allocation alpha in [0,1] between these two approaches, incorporating scale-dependent filter degradation, common-mode failures, and character fragility -- the risk that shaped behavior degrades or collapses under novel conditions. Under a multiplicative Pareto damage model, we derive closed-form expected harm and supplement it with tail-risk (CVaR) analysis via Monte Carlo simulation. Across three scenarios (optimistic, moderate, pessimistic), the optimal alpha* is interior or at the rules-only boundary and shifts weakly toward character shaping as deployment scale T grows, from negligible (Delta alpha* = +0.01) to pronounced (Delta alpha* = +0.21) depending on scenario. The dominant parameter is the baseline character fragility rate p^(0)_frag, which shifts alpha* by 0.50 across its range -- far exceeding the effect of tail severity, filter quality, or common-mode failure probability. CVaR and expected-harm optima converge at large T. These results suggest that safety architecture decisions depend less on deployment scale per se than on the reliability of character shaping under distributional shift.

</details>


### [76] [Jointly Predicting Courses and Grades Using a Transformer-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13409)
*Paul Savala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE（TRansformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation）模型，将学生的学术历史按学期编码，联合预测学生下学期的选课集合及对应成绩。在十年机构数据上训练后，联合预测模型相较仅预测成绩的相同架构将平均绝对误差降低近50%，并优于LSTM和GNN基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习分析中的预测模型将学生学术历史视为简单序列，忽略了同一学期内多门课程并修（concurrency）的特性。这种简化会导致对课业负担重或课程挑战大的学生预测不准确。

Method: TRACE模型采用Transformer架构，按学期（per-semester）对课程进行编码以捕捉课程并修效应；设计了一个新颖的损失函数，将课程集合预测与课程成绩预测相结合，实现联合学习。模型还支持自然融入学生属性数据。

Result: 在十年机构数据上训练的联合预测模型相较仅预测成绩的相同架构，平均绝对误差降低近50%；模型性能优于传统的LSTM序列模型和基于图神经网络的方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了现代神经架构在构建可解释模型方面的价值，模型可通过重训练和重新校准适配新机构，并可集成到高等教育机构的早期预警系统中。

Abstract: Existing predictive models in learning analytics often treat student academic history as a simple sequence, overlooking the concurrent nature of courses taken within a semester. This simplification can lead to inaccurate performance predictions, particularly for students with heavy or challenging course loads. This paper introduces a TRansformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation (TRACE) that addresses this limitation by jointly predicting both the set of courses a student will take and their corresponding grades for an upcoming semester. Our approach encodes courses on a per-semester basis to capture the effects of course concurrency and utilizes a novel loss function combining course-set prediction with grade prediction. We demonstrate that predicting courses taken in addition to the grades in those courses leads to significant improvements in prediction quality. Trained on ten years of institutional data, our joint prediction model reduces mean absolute error by nearly 50% compared to an identical architecture that predicts grades alone. The model also outperforms traditional LSTM-based sequential models, as well as graph neural network-based approaches, and offers natural ways to incorporate student attribute data. This work demonstrates the utility of modern neural architectures for creating interpretable models that can be adapted to new institutions via retraining and recalibration, as well as the importance of key techniques, such as predicting courses taken during training. We discuss how this model could be incorporated into early detection systems at institutions of higher education.

</details>


### [77] [Who Speaks Matters: Authority-Aware Multi-View RAG over Italian Parliamentary Proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13410)
*Mirko Tritella,Riccardo Pozzi,Matteo Palmonari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ParliamentRAG是为意大利众议院设计的检索增强生成系统，通过主题依赖的权威模型来解决议会记录检索中的三大风险（高频演讲者主导、无法按主题专长加权、政治敏感文本中的引文归属错误）。该系统在15个政策主题上与Google NotebookLM对比，在政治群体覆盖率、引文忠实度和专家偏好等维度上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 议会程序是民主审议的主要记录，但其体量庞大且碎片化，使公民、记者和研究者难以进行多视角访问。将RAG应用于议会记录存在三个具体风险：最频繁发言者的主导地位、无法根据主题专长对发言者加权、以及政治敏感文本中的引文错误归属，这些风险需要联合解决。

Method: 提出ParliamentRAG系统，其核心贡献是基于主题的权威模型，该模型根据当前查询估计每位发言者的权威度，结合可解释组件如职业、教育背景和先前发言记录。系统流程包括：根据用户查询检索相关发言片段、跨议会组织识别主题相关专家、生成综合各视角的摘要并附支持性引用。评估采用双层协议，结合自动化指标和6位领域专家的盲法A/B人工评估。

Result: 与Google NotebookLM对比，ParliamentRAG在15个政策主题上取得：政治群体覆盖率更高（0.97 vs 0.95）、完美的引文忠实度（1.00 vs 0.95）、在来源相关维度获得更强的专家偏好；而NotebookLM在散文/文风类维度上表现更强。

Conclusion: ParliamentRAG通过主题依赖的权威建模有效解决了议会RAG系统面临的多重风险，在来源可信度和多视角覆盖方面优于商业基线系统，但在文本质量维度仍有提升空间，说明两系统各有优势。

Abstract: Parliamentary proceedings are a primary record of democratic deliberation, yet their volume and fragmentation make multi-perspective access difficult for citizens, journalists, and researchers. Applying Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to parliamentary transcripts introduces three specific risks: dominance of the most frequent speakers, inability to weight speakers according to topical expertise, and citation misattribution in politically sensitive text. We present ParliamentRAG, a RAG system for the Italian Chamber of Deputies that addresses these risks jointly. Its core contribution is a topic-dependent authority model that estimates each speaker's authority as a function of the current query, combining interpretable components such as profession, education, and previous interventions. Given a user query, the system retrieves relevant speech chunks, identifies topic-relevant experts across parliamentary groups, and generates a summary synthesizing their perspectives, accompanied by supporting quotations. ParliamentRAG is evaluated against Google NotebookLM on 15 policy topics via a two-level protocol combining automated metrics and blind A/B human evaluation by six domain experts. The system achieves higher coverage across political groups (0.97 vs. 0.95), perfect quotation faithfulness (1.00 vs. 0.95), and stronger expert preferences on source-related dimensions, while NotebookLM remains stronger on prose-oriented dimensions.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Final Scores: A Systematic Evaluation of Agents for Long-Horizon AI Research and Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13417)
*Yiwei Li,Wanli Yang,Hexiang Tan,Xiangzhou Huang,Zhengyu Chen,Ziran Li,Borun Chen,Shanglin Lei,Huaisheng Zhu,Hao Tian,Fei Sun,Xunliang Cai,Jingang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对7个前沿模型在36个长时程任务上进行了系统评估，提出了一种基于规则指标的新框架，从解决方案构建、执行和反馈控制三个维度刻画模型运行中行为，并通过受控对比评估经验在任务内和跨任务间的复用效果。研究发现当前智能体更像"工程优化器"而非"自主研究者"：能制定和实施实用方案，但运行间性能波动大、最强方案多是对既有技术的适配或组合、真正的方法论创新稀少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注最终得分，既无法揭示性能提升或损失的来源，也难以判断累积经验是否改善后续决策，因此需要超越最终分数的系统化评估方法。

Method: 提出新评估框架，使用基于规则的指标刻画运行中行为（Solution Framing、Execution、Feedback Control三个维度），并通过受控比较评估经验在任务内和跨任务间的复用效果；对7个前沿模型在36个长时程任务上进行系统评估。

Result: 当前智能体更偏向"工程优化器"而非"自主研究者"：能构建和实现实用方案，但运行间性能差异大，最强解决方案多是对既有技术的适配或组合，真正的方法论创新罕見。性能受多重因素影响，包括相似最终结果背后的不同过程瓶颈、经验复用既可能帮助也可能误导后续决策、以及影响性能稳定性的框架设计。

Conclusion: 这些发现为改进模型训练、推理时策略、经验管理和框架设计提供了具体方向。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly capable of improving models, systems, and other technical artifacts through long-horizon experimentation. To understand the current state of this capability, however, evaluation must go beyond final scores, which neither reveal where progress is gained or lost nor indicate whether accumulated experience improves later decisions. We therefore present a systematic evaluation of seven frontier models on 36 long-horizon tasks based on a new framework that uses rule-based metrics to characterize within-run behavior through Solution Framing, Execution, and Feedback Control and controlled comparisons to assess experience reuse within and across tasks. The results show that current agents operate more like engineering optimizers than fully autonomous researchers: they can formulate and implement practical solutions, but their performance varies substantially across runs, their strongest solutions mainly adapt or combine established techniques, and genuine methodological novelty remains rare. Detailed analysis reveals that observed performance is shaped by multiple factors, including distinct process bottlenecks behind similar final outcomes, experience reuse that can help or mislead subsequent decisions, and harness designs that affect performance stability. These findings suggest concrete directions for improving model training, inference-time strategies, experience management, and harness design.

</details>


### [79] [Enhancing Virtual Agents through SLMs and Edge-Computing: An Exploratory Evaluation of Think and Memory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13420)
*Aimilios Hadjiliasi,Louis Nisiotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索使用小语言模型（SLMs）在边缘计算环境中支持认知具身智能体架构（CEAA）的"思考"和"记忆"组件，在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上基于Qwen2.5模型开发并评估了一个边缘虚拟智能体网关系统，实验验证了路由精度、记忆读取性能和延迟，展示了SLM驱动的原型智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能虚拟智能体需要在复杂虚拟世界及元宇宙中作为持久、自适应、上下文感知的实体工作，但在认知实现上存在概念和技术挑战；现有蓝图和开发方法中，CEAA提供了面向实现的框架，但如何利用边缘计算和生成式AI语言模型的最新进展来支持其认知组件的边缘化运行仍待探索。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5系列不同尺寸模型，在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上开发了一个基于边缘的虚拟智能体网关系统，聚焦实现CEAA中的"Think"和"Memory"两个核心认知编排和持久化过程，并通过一系列仿真实验评估路由精度、记忆读取性能和延迟。

Result: 实验证明了SLM驱动的原型智能体系统能够部分实现CEAA的关键过程，在路由精度、记忆读取性能和延迟方面表现出可行性，使具身智能体的认知"大脑"能够在沉浸式虚拟世界中高效且上下文相关地运行。

Conclusion: SLM结合边缘计算可以有效支撑CEAA中认知组件的边缘化部署，为开发认知能力可高效运行于交互式虚拟世界的具身智能体提供了可行路径，同时为元宇宙场景下的智能体架构提供了实现方案。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent virtual agents are expected to operate as persistent, adaptive, and context-aware entities within complex virtual and Metaverse worlds. However, implementing cognitively capable agents in such environments is conceptually and technologically challenging. Among a range of blueprints and development approaches, the Cognitive Embodied Agent Architecture (CEAA) has been developed as an implementation-oriented framework for architecting components of perception, memory, reasoning, planning, and embodied action. Considering the recent advances in edge computing and generative AI language models, this paper explores the use of Small Language Models (SLMs) to support edge-based operation of selected CEAA components, focusing on "Think" and "Memory" as processes central to cognitive orchestration and persistence of virtual agents in interactive virtual worlds. An edge-based virtual agent gateway system was developed and evaluated on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using Qwen2.5 models of different sizes, exploring the system's capability to process service requests and handle memory-driven conversations. A series of simulation experiments evaluated routing accuracy, memory-read performance, and latency, demonstrating an SLM-driven prototype agent system that partially implements selected CEAA processes to support the development of embodied agents whose cognitive "brain" can operate efficiently and contextually for interactive experiences in immersive virtual worlds.

</details>


### [80] [RAIL: An Automatic Classifier of the Artificial Intelligence Readiness Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13428)
*Juan Irving Vasquez,Juan Terven,Laura-Ivoone Garay-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统一AI就绪度等级（AIRL）九级序数量表及RAIL（独立LLM专家组评估）分类器，使AI成熟度可从自然语言描述中自动判定，解决了现有框架异构、难以自动应用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术成熟度评估对投资决策、项目管理和政策监测至关重要，但现有就绪度框架（TRL、MLTRL、AI/数据就绪度维度模型）异构且难以自动应用：TRL缺少AI特定门控标准，MLTRL依赖内部过程工件访问权限，维度模型量表难以直接比较。

Method: 提出两个贡献：(1) 统一AIRL九级序数量表，基于环境证据阶梯并辅以维度上限（涵盖规格、数据存在性、数据质量、数据合法性、专家知识、算法成熟度）、通用性锚定规则及明确赋值纪律，使就绪度可仅从自然语言描述判定；(2) 提出RAIL（独立LLM专家组分类器），由1个证据代理和6个独立维度代理组成，每个代理为职责范围狭窄的大语言模型，通过确定性最小值规则聚合裁决，并由首席专家在不对称权威下审查（可确认或降低但不可超越上限提升推荐）。

Result: 该方法在多个研究工作分析中进行了测试，结果表明具有一致性，且避免了单体LLM分类器的高估问题。

Conclusion: AIRL与RAIL相结合，提供了一种可从自然语言描述自动、一致地判定AI技术就绪度的方案，通过多代理分工与不对称审查机制有效规避了单一LLM评估中的过度乐观偏差。

Abstract: Assessing the maturity of artificial intelligence technologies is essential for investment decisions, project management, and policy monitoring, yet the available readiness frameworks are heterogeneous and difficult to apply automatically: the adaptation of Technology Readiness Levels to AI lacks AI-specific gating criteria, the Machine Learning Technology Readiness Levels presuppose access to internal process artifacts, and AI/data readiness dimension models employ scales that resist direct comparison. This paper makes two contributions. First, we unify these three frameworks into the Unified AI Readiness Level (AIRL), a nine-level ordinal scale built on an environmental evidence ladder and complemented by dimensional caps (covering specification, data existence, data quality, data legality, expert knowledge, and algorithmic maturity) together with a generality-anchoring rule and explicit assignment disciplines, so that a readiness level becomes decidable from a natural-language description of the work alone. Second, we propose RAIL (Readiness Assessment via Independent LLM-experts), a panel-of-experts classifier that operationalizes the scale: one evidence agent and six independent dimension agents, each a large language model with a narrowly scoped mandate, deliver verdicts that a deterministic minimum rule aggregates and a chief expert reviews under asymmetric authority, confirming or lowering the panel's recommendation but never raising it above the caps. The method was tested in the analysis of several research works showing consistency and avoiding overestimation from monolithic LLM classifiers.

</details>


### [81] [Academic League of Artificial Intelligence - An Integrative Perspective of Teaching, Research, and Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13447)
*Alison R. Panisson,Maria Eduarda W. M. Vianna,Italo Firmino da Silva,Heitor Henrique da Silva,Rafaela Fernandes Savaris,Bernardo Pandolfi Costa,Martin Augusto Gagliotti Vigil,Jim Lau,Agenor Hentz,Andréa Sabedra Bordin,Alexandre Leopoldo Gonçalves,Roberto Rodrigues-Filho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西圣卡塔琳娜联邦大学人工智能学术联盟（LIA）所采用的组织框架，该框架通过以学生为中心、基于项目的教学方法整合教学、科研和大学拓展三大支柱，结合民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织，并通过竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库和具有社会影响的人工智能应用等代表性举措加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术联盟已成为促进课外教育和加强大学与社会融合的重要机制，但如何在工程和计算机教育中系统性地整合教学、科研和大学拓展这三大大学支柱，仍缺乏灵活且可复制的组织框架指导。

Method: 提出一个将民主治理、协作学习与动态项目组织相结合的组织框架，以学生为中心、项目为导向，并通过多种代表性举措（竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库、社会影响人工智能应用）进行实例说明。

Result: 该框架成功展示了如何在同一组织结构中开展多样化的教育、科研和拓展活动，同时促进领导力、科学产出、社区参与和知识保存，表明该框架是一种灵活且可复制的模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的组织框架为将大学三大支柱整合到工程和计算机教育中提供了实践指导，可作为学术联盟及类似学生组织的参考范式。

Abstract: Academic leagues have become important mechanisms for promoting extracurricular education and strengthening the integration between universities and society. This paper presents the organizational framework adopted by the Academic League of Artificial Intelligence (LIA) at the Federal University of Santa Catarina (UFSC), designed to integrate teaching, research, and university extension through a student-centered, project-based approach. The framework combines democratic governance, collaborative learning, and dynamic project organization to foster both technical and transversal competencies. The framework is illustrated through representative initiatives, including competition teams, study groups, open lectures, knowledge repositories, and AI-powered applications with social impact. These projects demonstrate how diverse educational, scientific, and extension activities can be developed within a common organizational structure while promoting leadership, scientific production, community engagement, and knowledge preservation. The reported experience indicates that the proposed framework provides a flexible and replicable model for integrating the three university pillars into engineering and computing education, offering practical guidance for academic leagues and similar student organizations.

</details>


### [82] [MARC v1: An Open-Source Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical AI Reasoning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13476)
*Saisha Shetty,Satvik Tripathi,Austin Lin,Colin Zhao,Theodore Kim,Don Enwerem,Jacinta Arnold,Shahriar Faghani,Tessa S Cook*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARC是一个开源多智能体推理与协调框架，用确定性多智能体编排替代单体LLM提示词，用于临床推理任务。框架通过角色分工（提取、推理、答案生成、评估）实现显式上下文传递和可追踪的中间输出，并引入Decomposer模块从自然语言描述自动生成任务专属提示词，支持API和本地CPU部署，配置完全由YAML驱动，无需编写代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体LLM提示词方法在临床推理中存在缺乏可解释性和难以定位失败环节的问题；同时提示词工程需要大量人工劳动，对临床领域专家而言门槛过高。作者旨在提供一个无需编程知识、可解释、可追溯失败来源的多智能体编排框架。

Method: 提出MARC框架，采用确定性多智能体编排，协调四个角色分工智能体（提取、推理、答案生成、评估），通过显式上下文传递和可追踪中间输出来实现分阶段失败归因；引入Decomposer模块从纯文本描述自动生成智能体提示词；框架支持API和本地CPU部署，全部配置通过YAML完成。

Result: 论文为框架描述性质，未给出具体实验结果。宣布框架已开源发布于GitHub（https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1），强调其模型无关性、可解释性和对无编程背景的临床专家友好性。

Conclusion: MARC通过确定性多智能体编排和自动提示词生成，降低了临床推理AI系统的使用门槛，增强了可解释性与可追溯性，为临床领域专家提供无需代码修改即可配置的开放解决方案。

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [83] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AlayaWorld的改进版本，基于"条件信号应与生成内容在潜在表征和时间结构上尽可能匹配"的原则，将基于深度扭曲的空间记忆替换为流式3D点缓存渲染器，并重新设计条件编码管线使视觉条件与生成视频共享同一因果VAE潜在空间及时间统计特性，共包含六项具体修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlayaWorld的先前版本中，条件信号的表征方式与生成内容在潜在空间和时间结构上存在不匹配，导致条件信息无法被高效利用。作者认为，为了提升条件视频生成的性能，条件信号应该在潜在表示和时间结构上尽可能与生成内容对齐。

Method: 在保持骨干架构、分块自回归生成方案和训练数据不变的前提下，实施六项修改：(1) 用运动感知的潜在条件替代静态帧图像条件；(2) 将重新渲染的空间记忆作为连续序列进行因果编码；(3) 在像素空间中对齐时间记忆窗口；(4) 采用硬记忆丢弃，即移除记忆token而非置零；(5) 统一训练和推理阶段的VAE编解码协议；(6) 移除相机AdaLN分支，视点控制完全通过重新渲染的空间条件提供。

Result: 论文为报告形式，未在摘要中给出具体定量结果，但指出通过上述重设计，条件信号在潜在表示与时间结构上与生成内容一致，从而提升模型的条件利用效率与生成质量。

Conclusion: 新版本AlayaWorld验证了"条件信号应与生成内容在潜在表示和时间结构上匹配"的设计原则，通过重设计条件管线（尤其是流式3D点缓存渲染器和统一VAE潜在空间）显著改进了条件信号的整合方式。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [84] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuoteBench是一个用于评估LLM编码智能体（Coding Agent）通过Bash接口发出命令时，命令生成与执行传输之间边界的基准。通过精确最终状态验证，在56个一次性任务上测量模型在"解析边界"下的表现，揭示了匹配分数掩盖了生成错误与执行层引入失败之间的根本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体通过可能序列化、包装和重新解析模型输出的接口发出Bash命令。仅凭匹配执行分数无法区分命令生成错误与生成后执行层引入的失败。现有的评估方法将匹配分数视为模型的内在属性，忽略了解析层（parser）对任务成功率的显著影响，导致对模型真实能力的错误估计。

Method: 构建QuoteBench基准：56个来自14个事件衍生（incident-derived）家族的一次性任务，使用精确最终状态验证。关键设计是跨越生成契约（generation contract）与执行传输（execution transport）的边界，引入一个故意未转义（unescaped）的解析器。通过在插值点（interpolation point）转义来复现原始路径结果，确保任何恢复都必须来自模型改变其生成行为，而非执行层的修复。在八个相同窗口配置下，重放相同回复并通过额外解析器评估，同时测试边界披露（disclosure）后的恢复效果。

Result: 在八个相同窗口配置下，重放相同回复并通过额外解析器将成功率降低了55.4至73.2个百分点；边界披露后，六个配置恢复了30.4至60.7个百分点，另两个配置恢复为零或略为负值。原始生成在模型前沿（frontier）接近饱和，边界适应是区分模型的关键。GPT-5.6-sol的匹配差距仅为-3.6点，但实际隐藏了-64.3点的损伤和+60.7点的补偿。部署配置会重新排序模型：26个可比配对中有一个明确反转，另有四个处于单任务边缘。

Conclusion: 对命令发出型智能体的评估应报告模型配置、生成契约、执行路径、运行工作点（operating point）和最终状态验证器，而非将匹配分数视为模型的内在属性。当前研究表明，边界适应能力而非原始生成能力才是区分前沿模型的关键因素。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


### [85] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniScientist，一个端到端的全模态AI科学家系统，能够直接从异构原始证据（图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构、轨迹、表格、公式、图谱）进行多学科研究。系统包含感知层和3个自主代理（构思、实验、写作），在确定性流水线中运行，通过代码实现新颖性筛选、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追溯性。在5个学科家族、36个真实数据案例上评估，全部从原始数据完成到编译稿的完整流程，平均论文得分6.3；与仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体相比，直接感知在7个评估维度全面获胜（85%的逐对比较胜率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统虽然工作流覆盖日益完整，但通常只基于文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要进行推理，无法访问对科学发现至关重要的空间、时间、跨通道和程序性关系。这限制了系统对真实证据的理解深度和科学发现的可靠性。

Method: OmniScientist采用端到端全模态架构，由感知层（处理异构原始证据）加上3个自主代理（ideation用于研究问题生成、experiment用于实验设计与执行、writeup用于文稿撰写）组成，在确定性流水线中运行。系统在代码层面执行idea、rigour和claim三类检查，确保新颖性筛选、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追溯性。

Result: 在36个真实数据案例（覆盖5个学科家族、4类科学证据类型、9种模态）上，系统全部完成从原始数据到编译稿的完整流程，平均论文得分6.3（以参考推理骨干）。与仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体配对比较中，直接感知在所有7个评估维度上均占优，胜出85%的头对头判断。

Conclusion: 生命周期级的感知对于基于证据的科学发现至关重要，全模态直接感知提供了通往广泛能力的AI科学家的可行路径。这一结果表明，仅在流程层面覆盖而非在证据层面全面感知不足以支撑真正证据驱动的科学发现。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [MV2: Multi-View Multi-Vehicle Driving Dataset for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12442)
*Sanjay Bhargav Dharavath,Hanvitha Saraswathi Mukkamala,Faizan Farooq Khan,Ioannis Kakogeorgiou,Aditya Arun,C V Jawahar,Zakaria Laskar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MV2（Multi-View Multi-Vehicle）数据集与基准，用于评估不同视角合成（NVS）模型在动态城市场景中大幅视角变化下的表现。该数据集包含来自汽车、滑板车和无人机三类同步采集设备的50个高质量场景、12000张图像，通过训练一个车辆的相机流并在另一个车辆上测试来评估NVS方法。基准测试表明，NVS性能随视角差异增大而下降，且前馈姿态估计方法明显落后于基于优化的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可微渲染和NVS方法在真实驾驶场景中应用面临挑战：真实驾驶环境的稀疏视角采集、动态物体干扰以及有限的多轨迹数据限制了模型性能。现有数据集多为单轨迹采集，无法对NVS模型在大视角变化下的泛化能力进行充分评估。

Method: 构建MV2数据集，采用汽车、滑板车和无人机三种同步采集设备，各自沿不同但同步的轨迹运行。通过Structure-from-Motion对序列进行配准，并使用手工像素级对应注释验证相机位姿。设计跨车辆相机流的训练-测试协议，以评估NVS方法在较大视角变化下的性能。

Result: 在MV2基准上评估多种最新NVS和相机位姿估计方法，发现NVS性能随视角差异增大而显著下降；前馈（feed-forward）位姿估计方法与基于优化的方法相比存在明显差距，表明MV2是驾驶场景NVS研究的严格测试平台。

Conclusion: MV2提供了首个面向驾驶场景的多视角多车辆NVS基准，能够有效评估模型在大视角变化下的鲁棒性，为实际驾驶场景中的NVS研究提供了重要的数据集和基准协议。

Abstract: Differentiable rendering has advanced novel view synthesis (NVS), yet applying it to real-world driving remains difficult due to sparse capture viewpoints, dynamic objects, and limited multi-trajectory data. We introduce the Multi-View Multi-Vehicle (MV2) dataset and benchmark for evaluating NVS models under large viewpoint changes in dynamic urban scenes. MV2 features synchronized captures from a car, scooter, and drone, each following distinct yet synchronized trajectories. Training NVS methods on one vehicle's camera stream and testing on another enables evaluation under substantially larger viewpoint variations than existing single-trajectory datasets. All sequences are registered via Structure-from-Motion and camera poses verified using manual pixel-level correspondence annotations, yielding 50 high-quality scenes with 12000 images. Benchmarking recent NVS and camera pose estimation methods shows that NVS performance degrades with increasing viewpoint disparity, and that feed-forward pose estimators notably lag behind optimization-based approaches, highlighting MV2 as a rigorous testbed for NVS in driving. The dataset, benchmark protocol, and project resources are available at https://mv2-dataset.github.io/.

</details>


### [87] [HIMEC: Directional Change Representation and Fixed-Interface Decoding for Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12502)
*Aysha Ashraf,Shaina Ashraf,Wafaa I. M. Hussin,Ali Haider,Zhi Lu,Zhenming Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HIMEC提出了一种结合方向性变化表示（DCR）与固定接口解码的遥感图像变化描述（RSICC）方法。DCR将位相图像的差异分为出现、消失和共享上下文三个方向流，通过可学习查询编码器将融合特征转换为视觉条件变化查询令牌，作为场景解码器唯一的样本相关记忆。训练时辅助短语解码器提供基于描述的监督，场景解码器在训练和推理期间保持相同接口（固定零输入）。实验表明，在LEVIR-CC上HIMEC达到CIDEr=142.81±0.60，优于直接融合特征记忆的139.51±3.40；在SECOND-CC上固定零输入和匹配状态条件分别达到75.67和76.99 CIDEr。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数RSICC方法直接将融合后的视觉特征作为条件喂给标题解码器，对中间变化结构（中间变化结构与解码器接口的一致性）研究不足。作者希望通过分离方向性变化表示、引入固定接口解码机制，并系统研究训练-推理状态不匹配（cascade中teacher-forced与自回归状态的余弦距离达0.69）对性能的影响，来改进RSICC的性能与训练-推理一致性。

Method: 1) 方向性变化表示（DCR）：将符号化差异分离为出现导向、消失导向和共享上下文的三个流后再融合。2) 可学习查询编码器：将融合表示转换为视觉条件的变化查询令牌，作为场景解码器唯一的样本相关记忆。3) 训练时辅助短语解码器：提供基于caption的辅助监督。4) 场景解码器采用固定零输入，确保训练与推理共享相同接口。5) 额外评估了局部到场景级联（local-to-scene cascade），对比训练时teacher-forced局部状态与推理时自回归状态这两种条件方式，并分析状态不匹配对性能的影响。

Result: 在LEVIR-CC上（三种子匹配比较），HIMEC达到CIDEr=142.81±0.60，优于直接融合特征记忆的139.51±3.40。在SECOND-CC上，固定零输入和匹配状态条件诊断分别达到75.67和76.99 CIDEr，远高于不匹配级联的60.77。改变的LEVIR-CC验证对上，teacher-forced与自回归状态的均值余弦距离为0.69，匹配状态条件可恢复大部分性能损失，而置换状态对应关系无显著影响。

Conclusion: HIMEC通过方向性变化表示和固定接口解码显著提升了RSICC性能，证明了中间变化结构建模与训练-推理接口一致性对变化描述任务的重要性。同时，级联实验中状态不匹配确实带来性能缺陷，匹配条件可恢复大部分损失，但这些发现仅限于所评估的级联架构。代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Remote sensing image change captioning (RSICC) converts bitemporal imagery into a sentence describing semantic changes. Most RSICC methods condition caption decoders directly on fused visual features, leaving intermediate change structure and decoder-interface consistency less studied. We present HIMEC, combining Directional Change Representation (DCR) with fixed-interface decoding. DCR separates signed differences into appearance-oriented, disappearance-oriented, and shared-context streams before fusion. A learned-query encoder converts the fused representation into visually conditioned change-query tokens that form the scene decoder's only sample-dependent memory. A training-only auxiliary phrase decoder supplies caption-derived supervision. With a fixed zero input, the scene decoder maintains the same interface during training and inference. Separately, we evaluate a local-to-scene cascade conditioned on teacher-forced local states during training and autoregressive states at inference. On changed LEVIR-CC validation pairs, these states have a mean cosine distance of 0.69. Regime-matched conditioning recovers most of the associated deficit, whereas permuting state correspondence causes no detectable penalty. These findings are limited to the evaluated cascade. In a matched three-seed comparison, HIMEC reaches a Consensus-based Image Description Evaluation (CIDEr) score of $142.81\pm0.60$ on LEVIR-CC, versus $139.51\pm3.40$ for direct fused-feature memory. On SECOND-CC, fixed-zero and regime-matched diagnostic conditioning reach 75.67 and 76.99 CIDEr, respectively, versus 60.77 for the mismatched cascade. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/ayshaashra/HIMEC upon publication.

</details>


### [88] [Can Vision-Language Models Assess Proxemic Risk from Egocentric Robot Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12515)
*Vladyslava Rudas,Dmytro Kuzmenko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种开源视觉语言模型（VLM）——InternVL、Qwen-VL和SmolVLM在机器人自我中心视角图像上对四种危险等级进行分类的能力，比较了三种提示策略和两轮QLoRA微调。结果表明，未经微调时所有模型接近随机基线，微调仅带来适度提升；但Qwen-VL配合高级提示在高危险案例上实现了显著更高的召回率。进一步分析发现，正确的危险分类与更好的空间定位并不对应，说明模型可能在不关注场景相关区域的情况下产生有用的安全标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从机器人的自我中心视角评估近端危险（proxemic danger）对安全具身导航至关重要，需要视觉和上下文双重推理。现有VLM在细粒度近端推理和空间定位方面的能力尚不清楚，因此需要系统评估其在危险分类任务上的表现。

Method: 将机器人自我中心图像分类为四个危险等级，对三个开源VLM（InternVL、Qwen-VL、SmolVLM）采用三种提示策略和两轮QLoRA微调，并以分层随机基线作为对照；同时分析人员定位能力以检验危险分类与空间定位的对应关系。

Result: 未微调时所有模型表现接近基线；微调后整体仅获得适度改进。然而Qwen-VL配合高级提示在高危险情形下召回率显著高于其他模型。人员定位分析显示正确危险分类与更好的空间接地不对应，表明模型可能在未关注场景相关区域的情况下产生有用标签。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在细粒度近端推理和空间接地方面仍然受限，但针对性的提示工程和微调可以提升选定模型在高危险检测上的表现，为机器人安全导航中的VLM应用提供了方向性指导。

Abstract: Assessing proxemic danger from a robot's egocentric perspective is critical for safe embodied navigation in human environments and requires both visual and contextual reasoning. We evaluate three opensource vision-language models (VLMs) (\textit{InternVL}, \textit{Qwen-VL}, and \textit{SmolVLM}) on the classification of egocentric robot images into four danger levels, comparing three prompting strategies and two rounds of QLoRA fine-tuning against a stratified random baseline. Without fine-tuning, all models perform near the baseline, while fine-tuning yields only modest overall improvements. However, \textit{Qwen-VL} with an advanced prompt achieves substantially higher recall for high-danger cases than the other models. An analysis of person localization further shows that correct danger classification does not correspond to better spatial grounding, indicating that a model may produce a useful safety label without attending to the relevant region of the scene. These results show that current VLMs remain limited in fine-grained proxemic reasoning and spatial grounding, although targeted prompting and fine-tuning can improve high-danger detection in selected models.

</details>


### [89] [Surface-to-Skeleton 3D Cephalometry: Estimating Hidden Skeletal Landmarks from CT-Derived External Soft-Tissue Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12537)
*Tomoki Abe,Taiki Kanaya,Kazuki Saita,Mao Noda,Chie Tachiki,Yasushi Nishii,Hideo Saito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种坐标一致的表皮-骨骼映射任务，利用同一CT扫描中的外部软组织点云推断内部骨骼标志点。在240例来自两家医院的临床CT扫描上，使用分层点云模型在40例留出患者上实现骨骼标志点2.97mm、深部或表面不可见标志点3.03mm的平均径向误差，证实了从外部软组织几何推断CT内部骨骼标志点的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D面部标志点方法仅能定位可见皮肤表面的点，尚不明确能否从外部软组织几何推断CT定义的内在骨骼标志点。该问题的验证受制于光学到CT配准、扫描仪域和采集状态等混杂因素，需要设计可控实验进行隔离评估。

Method: 使用同一采集CT派生外部软组织点云与21个骨骼标志点及3个可见软组织标志点配对，构建锁定的回顾性协议。采用集成分层点云模型进行表面到骨架的坐标一致估计，将估计与光学-CT配准、扫描仪域、采集状态效应分离，并独立分析覆盖率影响。通过患者失配对照和覆盖率消融进行验证，并执行光学迁移诊断。

Result: 在40例留出患者上，骨骼标志点平均径向误差为2.97mm，深部或表面不可见标志点为3.03mm。患者失配对照表明存在超出固定群体配置或全局相似性的患者特异性信号；覆盖率消融显示对非前向几何的依赖；光学迁移诊断揭示存在大量覆盖率相关和全局构型分量，但可部署的光学推断仍未解决。

Conclusion: 该受控可行性问题得到肯定答案，为隐藏骨骼标志点推断提供了基础，同时也指出了光学部署场景中仍需解决的覆盖率与全局构型挑战。

Abstract: Existing 3D facial-landmark methods localize points on visible skin, but whether CT-defined internal skeletal landmarks can be inferred from external soft-tissue geometry remains unclear. We formulate a coordinate-consistent surface-to-skeleton task using same-acquisition CT-derived surfaces, separating estimation from optical-to-CT registration, scanner-domain, and acquisition-state effects, with coverage analyzed separately. From 240 clinical CT scans from two hospitals, we construct a locked retrospective protocol pairing CT-derived external soft-tissue point clouds with 21 skeletal landmarks and three visible soft-tissue landmarks. An integrated hierarchical point-cloud model achieves 2.97 mm mean radial error on skeletal landmarks and 3.03 mm on deep or surface-invisible landmarks in 40 held-out patients. Patient-mismatch controls support patient-specific signal beyond a fixed population configuration or global similarity alone, while coverage ablations indicate dependence on non-anterior geometry. Optical-transfer diagnostics reveal substantial coverage-related and global-configuration components, although deployable optical inference remains unresolved. These results answer the controlled feasibility question affirmatively and provide a basis for hidden skeletal landmark inference.

</details>


### [90] [StrAD: A Streaming Method and Benchmark for Audio Description Generation for Long-form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12549)
*Julian Spravil,Sebastian Houben,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StrAD基准和流式密集视频字幕方法，用于全片长视频的自动音频描述生成，不需要ground-truth时间戳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉内容作为主流交流媒介，缺乏音频描述（AD）使视障人群无法访问。手动创建AD成本高昂，覆盖率极低；现有自动AD方法依赖ground-truth时间戳和额外上下文线索（如角色数据库），现有基准也仅覆盖短片段且标注不匹配，缺乏对全片长视频AD生成的系统评估。

Method: 将AD生成重构为流式密集视频字幕任务。使用滑动窗口处理全长视频，将AD插入现有转录文本中而不依赖ground-truth时间戳；同时支持微调模型（StrAD-FT）和视觉-语言模型的zero-shot提示（StrAD-Zero）。构建涵盖电影、纪录片、短片、表演和游戏等多种类型的StrAD基准。

Result: 片段级任务上，StrAD-FT在CMD-AD上达36.3 CIDEr（超过Shot-by-shot 10.0），在StrAD上建立51.0 CIDEr的参考点，在MAD-Eval上达24.9 CIDEr。全视频流式任务上，StrAD-FT达到2.4 SODA，优于StrAD-Zero的1.1，但两者在时间定位和叙事连贯性上仍有限。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个流式AD生成方法，能在无ground-truth时间戳的情况下实时生成AD，使全视频AD生成的性能可测量，是可扩展访问性的前提条件。但仍存在时间定位和叙事连贯性方面的局限，需进一步改进。

Abstract: Visual content is the dominant medium of communication, yet without audio descriptions (ADs), it remains inaccessible to blind and low-vision people. ADs narrate context-relevant visual events during natural audio pauses. Manually creating ADs is expensive, limiting coverage to a small fraction of available content. Most existing automatic AD generation methods frame the task as video clip captioning, requiring ground-truth timestamps and additional context cues such as character databases. Current benchmarks reinforce this framing, consisting of short video segments paired with automatic or task-mismatched annotations. We introduce StrAD, a benchmark for long-form AD generation on full-length videos spanning diverse genres such as movies, documentaries, short films, performances, and video games. We reformulate AD generation as streaming dense video captioning. Our approach processes full-length videos with a sliding window, inserting ADs into existing transcripts without ground-truth timestamps, and supports both fine-tuned models and zero-shot prompting of vision-language models. On the segment-level task with given timestamps, our fine-tuned StrAD-FT sets the state of the art on CMD-AD with 36.3 CIDEr (+10.0 over Shot-by-shot), establishes a reference point on StrAD (51.0 CIDEr), and remains competitive on MAD-Eval at 24.9 CIDEr. On the full-video streaming task, StrAD-FT reaches a SODA score of 2.4 against 1.1 for our zero-shot baseline StrAD-Zero, though both exhibit limitations in temporal localization and narrative coherence. While prior work has tackled full-video AD generation in an offline, multi-stage fashion, ours is the first streaming approach, generating ADs on the fly without ground-truth timestamps. StrAD makes progress on full-video AD generation measurable, a prerequisite for scaling accessibility.

</details>


### [91] [Attribute-Conditioned Multimodal Slot Factorization for Controllable Fashion Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12570)
*Najmeh Forouzandehmehr,Topojoy Biswas,Evren Korpeoglu,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-slotgate，一种多模态槽编码器，将Fashion-CLIP的文本和图像嵌入分解为四个具名属性槽（类别、颜色、图案、人群），每个槽学习独立的文本-图像门控。在H&M数据集上，结合槽相似度和槽逻辑回归检索分数，达到0.7566 macro ConstraintSatisfied@10，显著优于等权多模态融合（0.7142）和纯文本检索（0.4755）。颜色属性提升最大（+0.568绝对提升），门控权重无监督地呈现出可解释性：颜色偏向图像证据（57.4%）、类别偏向文本、图案和人群居中。量化的槽码还支持定向干预（颜色15.3倍提升）且无额外信息泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时尚检索中的单一嵌入向量将类别、颜色、图案、人群等多重属性信号混合在一起，无法在检索时进行属性级别的独立控制。已有的语义ID方法虽提供离散条目码，但这些码通常是条目级或残差地址优化的，不暴露具名、可独立控制的属性槽。因此需要一种能够将多属性信号显式分解到独立属性槽的方法。

Method: MM-slotgate是一种多模态槽编码器，将Fashion-CLIP文本和图像嵌入分解为四个具名属性槽（类别、颜色、图案、人群）。每个槽学习一个独立的文本-图像门控机制：视觉锚定属性（颜色、图案）可更多依赖图像证据，而分类学导向属性（类别、人群）保持文本驱动。检索时使用组合的槽相似度与槽逻辑回归（slot-logit）评分。

Result: 在H&M数据集上，MM-slotgate达到0.7566 macro ConstraintSatisfied@10，优于等权多模态融合（0.7142）和纯文本检索（0.4755）。颜色属性最大提升（0.321→0.889，+0.568），这是因为学习的颜色门控分配了57.4%的图像证据权重。门控具有可解释性：颜色偏向图像、类别偏向文本、图案和人群居中。线性探测显示无超出标签相关基线的额外泄漏；量化槽码支持定向干预，颜色属性获得15.3倍提升。

Conclusion: 可控时尚检索受益于类型化、属性条件化的多模态槽，而非单一全局嵌入或不透明的条目级语义ID。槽级门控带来的可解释属性分解使检索更加精确可控。

Abstract: Fashion retrieval often requires satisfying multiple attributes at once, such as category, color, pattern, and demographic. Monolithic embeddings mix these signals into a single vector, making attribute-specific control difficult at retrieval time. Many existing semantic-ID methods provide discrete item codes, but these codes are typically optimized as item-level or residual addresses and do not expose named, independently controllable attribute slots.
  We introduce MM-slotgate, a multimodal slot encoder that factorizes Fashion-CLIP text and image embeddings into four named attribute slots. Each slot learns its own text-image gate, so visually grounded attributes such as color and pattern can rely more on image evidence, while taxonomy-oriented attributes such as category and demographic can remain more text-driven.
  On H&M, using a combined slot-similarity and slot-logit retrieval score, MM-slotgate achieves 0.7566 macro ConstraintSatisfied@10, outperforming equal-weight multimodal fusion (0.7142) and fCLIP text-only retrieval (0.4755). The largest gain is on color, which improves from 0.321 to 0.889 (+0.568 absolute), as the learned color gate assigns 57.4% weight to image evidence. The learned gates are interpretable without modality supervision: color is image-leaning, category is text-leaning, and pattern and demographic lie near the middle.
  The resulting slots also remain controllable: linear probes show no measured excess leakage beyond the label-correlation baseline, and quantized slot codes support targeted intervention, including a 15.3x lift for color. These results suggest that controllable fashion retrieval benefits from typed, attribute-conditioned multimodal slots rather than either a single global embedding or opaque item-level semantic IDs.
