Result: 通过对比50多项已有工作（涵盖智能体协议、去中心化身份、OAuth/OIDC扩展、零信任治理、委派和审计架构），表明没有任何先前架构将持久身份、能力感知发现、信任协商与问责统一为四层信任基底以支持安全的智能体互操作。

Conclusion: InterSAGE作为信任语义与通信协议解耦的补充性安全基底，允许通信协议独立演进，同时保持信任语义的显式化、可移植性和可验证性，填补了智能体互操作中安全信任原语的空白。

Abstract: The emerging Internet of Agents enables LLM-powered agents to discover peers, invoke tools, and delegate tasks across organizational boundaries. Existing protocols increasingly define how agents exchange messages, but not how an agent proves its identity, authorization, advertised capabilities, or accountability after delegation. We present InterSAGE, a trust-native protocol suite that supplies this missing security substrate alongside, rather than in place of, communication protocols. InterSAGE comprises four layers: Persistent Identity, Discovery, Trust Negotiation, and Accountability. Its four core primitives are: (1) Agent Identity Cards that bind developer, code package, operator, and deployment context; (2) capability-aware discovery using DID-bound Verifiable Credential manifests; (3) trust negotiation combining monotonic capability attenuation with two-tier access control; and (4) kernel-mediated cryptographic audit trails that bind usage, delegation, and execution traces to agent identity without a consensus ledger. InterSAGE is designed to complement MCP, A2A, ANP, and AG-UI, allowing communication protocols to evolve independently while keeping trust semantics explicit, portable, and verifiable. We compare InterSAGE with more than 50 efforts spanning agent protocols, decentralized identity, OAuth/OIDC extensions, zero-trust governance, delegation, and audit architectures. We show that no prior architecture jointly enforces persistent identity, capability-aware discovery, trust negotiation, and accountability as a unified four-layer trust substrate for secure agent interoperability.

</details>


### [192] [InSPECtor: Improving SLEIGH Processor Specification Veracity via Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13042)
*Michael Chesser,Paul Quirk,Douglas Cooke,Guy Farrelly,Surya Nepal,Damith C. Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了对开源SLEIGH处理器规范（Ghidra使用）进行系统化验证的框架InSPECtor，通过对x86-64、AArch64、ARM/Thumb、RISC-V和MSP430等多样化的开源规范进行差分测试，发现了超过38,920处不一致，提炼出125个独特bug（含修复建议），并提出了8条改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理器规范支撑着反汇编器、反编译器、模拟器等关键安全和程序分析工具，但它们的正确性很少被检验。规范中的错误会扭曲程序行为、掩盖漏洞并促成分析规避技术，因此亟需对这些规范进行系统性验证。

Method: 设计并实现了基于代理式自动oracle验证策略的测试框架InSPECtor。该方法利用规范本身的结构来枚举可解码的指令形式并生成有针对性的初始状态，然后通过比较使用该处理器规范进行模拟的模拟器与硬件参考实现，对指令的解码和模拟进行差分测试，以发现规范中的错误。

Result: 在x86-64、AArch64、ARM/Thumb、RISC-V、MSP430等多个开源规范（涵盖不同规范风格、作者偏好和指令集架构设计）上，发现了超过38,920处差异，提炼出125个独特bug并提供了修复方案，识别了解码缺陷、语义缺陷以及跨厂商不一致问题，最终总结出8条具体建议。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了处理器规范正确性的重要性，提供了一个实用的工具来显著提高SLEIGH处理器规范的保真度，从而增强下游安全和分析工具的可靠性。

Abstract: Processor specifications underpin critical security and program- analysis tools such as disassemblers, decompilers, and emulators, yet, their correctness is rarely examined. Errors in specifications distort program behaviour, obscure vulnerabilities, and enable analysis-evasion techniques. Validating processor specifications is a non-trivial task. Our study is a significant undertaking to enable, for the first time, the systematic validation of open-source SLEIGH language specifications, predominantly used by Ghidra. We design and implement a testing framework based on an automated oracle validation strategy by proxy. Our approach leverages the structure encoded in a specification itself to enumerate decodable instruction forms and generate targeted initial states. Then differentially test the successful decoding and emulation of those instructions by comparing emulators exercising the processor specification against hardware references.
  Applying InSPECtor across diverse, open-source specifications---x86-64, AArch64, ARM/Thumb, RISC-V, MSP430---embedding differences in specification styles, author preferences, and instruction set architecture designs, we uncovered over 38,920 discrepancies that led to 125 unique bugs with proposed fixes, identifying decoding and semantic defects as well as cross-vendor inconsistencies. We distill our findings into 8 concrete recommendations to drive future improvements. Our work underscores the importance of specification correctness and provides a practical tool to substantially improve the fidelity of SLEIGH processor specifications, strengthening the reliability of downstream security and analysis tools.

</details>


### [193] [Operationalizing Cyber Threat Intelligence with GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13050)
*Atul Kabra,Prakhar Paliwal,Manjesh K. Hanawal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文探讨在自动生成检测规则时，知识图谱检索系统（Microsoft GraphRAG）相比传统向量相似度检索（Naive RAG）是否能产生更依赖持久性指标（金字塔顶端线索）的检测计划。案例研究显示GraphRAG方案在旋转所有IP、域名和文件哈希后仍保持100%检测率，而Naive RAG仅剩29%；跨9份真实CTI报告的实验证实了相同结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全研究人员发布网络攻击报告后，检测工程师需将其转化为可工作的检测规则。然而现有自动化尝试仅提取简单线索（IP、域名、文件哈希）转为封禁列表，这些指标极易被攻击者快速更换，导致检测规则部署后迅速失效（即"疼痛金字塔"理念）。作者希望解决这一自动化生成检测规则质量低、易规避的根本问题。

Method: 将同一份CTI报告分别输入两个检索系统：Microsoft GraphRAG（知识图谱检索）和Naive RAG（标准向量相似度检索），两者使用相同的报告、相同的生成指令和相同的语言模型编写最终检测计划，仅检索环节不同。通过单案例详细研究（APT28报告）以及跨9份来自四家供应商的真实CTI报告的重复比较实验进行评估。

Result: 在APT28案例中，旋转报告中的所有IP地址、域名和文件哈希后，GraphRAG方案保持100%的检测触发率，而Naive RAG方案仅保留29%。在9份报告的重复比较中，GraphRAG计划始终触及疼痛金字塔中更高、更难以规避的层次，即便两个系统总得分接近时亦然。

Conclusion: 研究支持将知识图谱感知检索视为自动生成SOC可部署狩猎计划的架构正确基础。同时指出生成提示词（prompt）的措辞对结果的影响几乎与检索后端本身同等重要。

Abstract: When a security researcher publishes a report on a cyberattack, detection engineers are supposed to turn it into working detection rules. In practice, most automated attempts at this only extract the simplest clues from the report --- bad IP addresses, domain names, and file hashes --- and turn them into block lists. This is a weak strategy, because attackers can change these simple clues within hours or days, so the resulting detections stop working almost as soon as they are deployed. Security teams describe this idea with the Pyramid of Pain. This project asks whether feeding a report into a knowledge-graph retrieval system, Microsoft GraphRAG, rather than a standard vector-similarity retrieval system (Naive RAG), produces detection plans that rely more on these durable, top-of-pyramid clues. Both systems are given the same report, the same generation instructions, and the same language model to write the final plan; only the retrieval step differs. In a detailed case study of one APT28 report, the GraphRAG plan kept firing at 100\% of its detections after every IP address, domain, and file hash in the report was rotated, while the Naive RAG plan kept firing at only 29\%. Repeating the comparison across nine real CTI reports from four vendors confirms the same pattern: GraphRAG plans consistently reach higher, harder-to-evade levels of the pyramid, even when the two systems end up close on total score. The results support treating knowledge-graph-aware retrieval as the architecturally correct foundation for automatically generating SOC-deployable hunting plans, while showing that the wording of the generation prompt matters almost as much as the retrieval back-end itself.

</details>


### [194] [A Commitment-Based Hybrid Post-Quantum Cryptographic Model for Multi-File Cloud Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13138)
*Lemdi Frank Prikutse,Regina Esi Turkson,Alimatu-Saadia Yussiff,Abdul-Lateef Yussiff,Maame G. Asante-Mensah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于承诺的混合后量子认证模型，通过SHA3-256批量承诺将多个文件的签名次数从n次减少到1次，在不牺牲安全性的前提下显著降低了批量上传场景中的认证开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云存储客户端需要抵御未来量子计算攻击的认证方案，通常采用经典密码学原语与标准化后量子算法的混合构造。然而，朴素地扩展到多文件上传场景时，每个文件都需要一次格基签名，导致认证时间随批量大小线性增长，在实际批量规模下开销过大。

Method: 该模型包含三个核心组件：AES-256-GCM批量加密、X25519与ML-KEM-768混合密钥封装机制、以及Ed25519与ML-DSA-65混合双签名。关键创新在于对所有密文计算SHA3-256批量承诺，生成单个固定大小的摘要，只需对该摘要签名一次，将每批签名调用次数从n次降为1次。

Result: 在通用客户端平台上经过20次重复实验，该模型使签名阶段耗时在批量增长时保持近似恒定，而传统的逐文件基线则线性增长。在n=1000时，对100 KB、1 MB和10 MB文件分别实现了629倍、606倍和725倍的签名阶段耗时缩减。

Conclusion: 基于承诺的混合后量子模型通过将昂贵的公钥签名操作从逐文件摊销到单次批量签名，有效解决了批量子认证的计算瓶颈，为大规模云存储批量上传提供了实用的后量子安全认证方案。

Abstract: Cloud storage clients increasingly require authentication that remains secure against future quantum-capable adversaries, motivating hybrid constructions that combine classical primitives with standardized post-quantum alternatives. Extended naively to multi-file upload, such constructions incur a per-file lattice signing cost that dominates authentication time and becomes prohibitive at realistic batch sizes. This paper presents a commitment-based hybrid post-quantum model that addresses this bottleneck. It comprises AES-256-GCM bulk encryption, a hybrid X25519 with ML-KEM-768 key encapsulation mechanism, and a hybrid Ed25519 with ML-DSA-65 dual signature, computed over a SHA3-256 batch commitment. The commitment binds all ciphertexts in a batch to a single fixed-size digest that is signed once, reducing the number of post-quantum signature invocations per batch from n to one, independent of batch size; the remaining encryption and hashing is bounded by fast symmetric throughput. On a commodity client platform, averaged over 20 repetitions, this holds signing-phase time near-constant as the batch grows while the per-file baseline scales linearly. At n = 1000, the model reduces signing-phase time by factors of 629, 606, and 725 for 100 KB, 1 MB, and 10 MB files respectively, against a per-file dual-signing baseline sharing every other primitive.

</details>


### [195] [Smart Contract Invariants Protect Against Cybercriminals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13191)
*Sofia Bobadilla,Humaira Afrin,Angela Novelli,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文主张程序不变量是解决区块链智能合约安全问题的强有力手段，通过INVARIANTEVAL基准（28个真实以太坊攻击事件）和PONDEREPLAY重放框架（重放108,637笔历史交易）验证了智能合约不变量能够阻断所有测试的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链是计算领域中最具对抗性的环境，大量资金被网络犯罪分子利用漏洞窃取，目前尚无概念或技术能真正有效解决这一问题，亟需找到可靠的安全防护方案。

Method: 提出"智能合约不变量"作为核心防护概念，设计原始实验协议：1) 研究不变量如何抵御过去真实发生的攻击；2) 评估自动化工具能否找到这些不变量。构建INVARIANTEVAL基准（28个真实以太坊攻击及对应人工编写的不变量），并开发PONDEREPLAY重放框架通过重新执行交易来验证不变量的正确性和可靠性。

Result: 智能合约不变量成功阻断了INVARIANTEVAL中所有28个攻击案例，通过重放108,637笔历史交易得到充分验证。

Conclusion: 大规模实验充分证明智能合约不变量能够有效抵御网络犯罪攻击，是解决区块链安全问题的一条可行且强大的路径。

Abstract: Blockchains are among the most adversarial environments in computing. Billions are stolen by cybercriminals who exploit vulnerabilities. This is an open problem and no concept or technique has proven to really make a difference. In this paper, we claim that the classical notion of program invariant is perhaps the most powerful solution to the problem. We devise anoriginal experimental protocol to 1) study how invariants would have protected against past real-world attacks and 2) whether state-of-the-art automated tools can find them. The experimental toolchain is sophisticated. It is based on INVARIANTEVAL, a benchmark of 28 real Ethereum exploits, each paired with a human-authored invariant that blocks the attack. We validate every invariant with PONDEREPLAY, a replay framework that re-executes transactions in order to prove the correctness and soundness of smart contract invariants. We demonstrate that smart contract invariants block all the cybercriminal attacks in INVARIANTEVAL, fully validated by replaying 108,637 historical transactions. Our large-scale experiments clearly demonstrate that smart contract invariants protect against cybercriminals.

</details>


### [196] [Slow and Steady: Preventing MEV with Verifiable Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13271)
*Zeta Avarikioti,Dimitris Karakostas,Karl Kreder,Shreekara Shastry*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对分布式账本中最大可提取价值（MEV）机会的防御机制，通过在生成交易时强制引入可验证延迟，使区块创建者无法在不破坏活性（liveness）的情况下对MEV机会做出反应。作者在拜占庭环境和博弈论理性参与者模型下均给出了正面结果，同时提出了限制该防御线路上限的负面界，并通过历史MEV数据的实际部署验证了机制的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV（最大可提取值）是分布式账本（如区块链）中区块创建者/验证者通过重新排序、插入或审查交易来提取额外经济价值的现象，对系统的公平性和安全性构成严重威胁。现有防御手段往往面临实现复杂或效果有限的问题，因此需要一种新的机制来有效缓解MEV攻击。

Method: 该防御机制的核心思想是在交易生成阶段强制执行可验证延迟（verifiable delay），使得区块创建者无法及时对MEV机会的出现做出反应，从而在不破坏系统活性（liveness）的前提下消除MEV利用的可能性。论文分别在拜占庭容错模型（面对恶意参与者）和博弈论模型（面对理性参与者）中对该机制进行了形式化分析与验证。

Result: 论文在拜占庭设置和理性参与者的博弈论模型中都获得了正面结果，表明该机制在这些场景下是有效的。同时提出了负面界（negative bounds），揭示了这种防御思路的内在局限性。此外，基于历史MEV数据的实证分析表明，该机制在现实部署中可以有效阻止大部分现有的MEV威胁。

Conclusion: 基于可验证延迟的防御机制为MEV问题提供了一种有前景且实用的解决方案，在理论模型和实际数据层面均展现了有效性，同时也明确了该方法的能力边界，为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Our work presents a defense mechanism against Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) opportunities in distributed ledgers. The mechanism relies on the idea of enforcing a verifiable delay when generating transactions, such that a block creator cannot react to the appearance of a MEV opportunity without breaking liveness. We present positive results both in the Byzantine setting and in a game theoretic model of rational participants. We additionally present negative bounds that outline the limitations of this line of defense. Finally, we explore real-world implementation details of verifiable delays and show that, based on historical MEV data, our mechanism could realistically help prevent most existing MEV threats.

</details>


### [197] [VR-Themis: A Scalable Framework for Virtual Reality Application Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13290)
*Gengyang Xu,Hanyang Guo,Hong-Ning Dai,Weizhi Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VR-Themis，一个基于Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour（HOB）的两阶段VR应用克隆检测框架，通过粗粒度聚类和细粒度HOB指标相似度计算，在4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用且无误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用打包（应用克隆）严重威胁用户安全和开发者版权，但现有检测方法主要面向Android等移动平台，未能捕捉VR应用的核心特征，因此无法有效检测VR市场中的克隆应用。

Method: 提出两阶段检测框架VR-Themis：第一阶段为粗粒度阶段，基于可检索的统计特征对应用进行聚类，以实现大规模VR应用数据集的可扩展性；第二阶段为细粒度阶段，利用定义的HOB（Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour）指标对第一阶段识别出的可疑应用进行深度相似度分析。

Result: 在收集的4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用，且没有误报，验证了该框架的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: VR-Themis能够有效且可扩展地检测VR应用克隆，弥补了现有移动平台克隆检测方法在VR场景下的不足。

Abstract: Repackaging of mobile applications (aka app cloning) not only threatens the security and privacy of mobile users but also infringes upon the copyright of the original app developers. However, existing detection methods that primarily focus on mobile platforms (such as Android) fail to capture the essential features of virtual reality (VR). Consequently, they are inadequate for effectively detecting cloned VR apps, which have often been targeted by illegal users in the VR market. Considering the unique features of VR apps, this paper proposes a two-stage app clone detection framework, namely VR-Themis, based on \emph{Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour} (HOB). Firstly, VR-Themis exploits the coarse-grained stage to cluster apps based on their retrievable statistical features, making this tool scalable to large-scale VR app datasets. Then, in the fine-grained stage, VR-Themis performs in-depth analysis of the suspicious apps (identified in the first stage) by calculating similarity using our defined \emph{HOB metrics}. Our extensive experiments indicate that VR-Themis successfully detects 307 suspected clone apps from the collected 4,277 VR apps without false positives, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [198] [TeleGapper: On the (un)reliability of Privacy Policies in Telegram Mini apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13390)
*Luca Ferrari,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了TeleGapper，一个黑盒动态分析框架，用于评估Telegram Mini Apps的隐私态势。通过对tApps Center收集的278个Mini Apps进行评估，发现59.4%的应用会联系至少一个未披露的第三方，78.8%的应用仅依赖Telegram默认隐私政策，且没有任何应用提供用户同意或退出机制，揭示了该生态系统中严重的透明度和合规性缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram Mini Apps作为嵌入在Telegram客户端中的Web应用，形成了广泛的第三方服务生态系统，但其隐私属性尚未得到充分研究。与微信等采用严格管控的专有执行框架不同，Telegram的Mini Apps在WebView中运行，结合了平台提供的上下文、标准Web能力和不受限制的出站网络访问，使得敏感信息可能通过普通Web请求被发送给分析、广告、追踪等第三方，且可见性有限。因此需要系统性地研究这一生态系统的隐私现状。

Method: TeleGapper采用黑盒动态分析方法：捕获Mini Apps运行时的网络流量，识别第三方通信，并将观察到的数据流与隐私披露信息进行对比。具体而言，从tApps Center（一个社区驱动的Telegram第三方应用发现目录）收集了278个可用的Mini Apps作为评估样本。

Result: 评估结果显示：59.4%的Mini Apps至少联系一个未披露的第三方；78.8%的应用完全依赖Telegram的默认隐私政策（而非自定义政策）；没有任何应用提供用户同意或选择退出机制。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了Telegram Mini Apps生态系统中存在显著的透明度和合规性差距。尽管该平台被广泛使用，但其隐私保障机制存在明显不足，尤其是默认隐私政策可能包含笼统的表述而无法准确反映各Mini App的实际数据实践，整体上缺乏用户同意机制进一步加剧了隐私风险。

Abstract: Telegram Mini Apps are Web applications embedded within the Telegram client, forming an ecosystem of third-party services within one of the world's most widely used messaging platforms. Despite their growing adoption and access to Telegram-provided context, their privacy properties remain largely unexplored. Unlike ecosystems such as WeChat, which rely on tightly controlled, proprietary execution frameworks, Telegram adopts a different model: Mini Apps run inside a WebView, combining platform-provided context with standard Web capabilities and unrestricted outbound networking. This enables applications to transmit sensitive information to analytics, advertising, tracking, or other third parties through ordinary Web requests, often with limited visibility.
  Privacy disclosures are therefore critical for transparency. Telegram allows Mini Apps either to define an application-specific privacy policy or to rely on a platform-provided default policy. While the latter reduces the developer's disclosure burden, it may lead to generic statements that do not accurately capture actual data practices of individual Mini Apps.
  In this paper, we present TeleGapper, a black-box dynamic analysis framework to assess the privacy posture of Mini Apps by capturing runtime network traffic, identifying third-party communications, and comparing observed data flows against disclosed privacy information. We evaluate 278 working Mini Apps collected from tApps Center, a community-driven catalogue for discovering third-party applications in Telegram. We find that 59.4% contact at least one undisclosed third party, 78.8% rely exclusively on Telegram's default privacy policy, and none provides a consent or opt-out mechanism. These findings expose a substantial transparency and compliance gap in a widely used yet understudied ecosystem.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Black-Box Knowledge Transfer across Distinct Feature Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12403)
*Oh-Ran Kwon,Daeyoung Ham*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从预训练黑盒预测函数向异构输入空间迁移预测知识的方法，通过将目标回归函数分解为可迁移与不可迁移两部分，利用两阶段神经网络分别估计，并在多个黑盒场景下支持聚合以降低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练黑盒预测函数蕴含了大规模数据集和大量计算蒸馏出的知识，但当可用输入特征与黑盒期望的特征不一致时，无法直接使用这些知识。需要在异构输入空间之间迁移预测知识。

Method: 将目标回归函数分解为可迁移组件（由黑盒可提供信息）和不可迁移组件（捕捉新空间特有信息）。提出两阶段神经网络流程：第一阶段利用丰富的无标注特征对（连接两个输入空间）估计可迁移组件；第二阶段利用有限标签估计不可迁移组件。扩展至多个黑盒场景，支持聚合多个黑盒的预测。

Result: 推导出预测风险界，当不可迁移组件较小或平滑时，优于无迁移方案的风险界，且方法能自适应两种情形。在额外条件下，估计量的最坏情况风险在多项式阶上严格小于仅使用标签数据的极小极大风险。多黑盒聚合可降低预测误差。

Conclusion: 该方法通过分解可迁移与不可迁移组件，实现了从黑盒到异构输入空间的预测知识迁移，在理论与实证上均体现了实用价值，且支持多黑盒聚合进一步优化预测性能。

Abstract: Pre-trained black-box predictive functions encode knowledge distilled from massive datasets and extensive computation. However, when the available input features differ from those the black box expects, direct use is infeasible. We introduce a method for transferring predictive knowledge from the black box to a new, heterogeneous input space. Our approach decomposes the target regression function into a transferable component, which the black box can inform, and a non-transferable component, which captures information unique to the new space. We propose a two-step neural network procedure, estimating the transferable component from abundant unlabeled feature pairs that bridge the two input spaces and the non-transferable component from limited labels. We derive prediction risk bounds that improve on those of a non-transfer alternative when the non-transferable component is small or smooth, and the procedure adapts to either case. Under additional conditions, the worst-case risk of our estimator is of strictly smaller polynomial order than the minimax risk of estimation from the labeled data alone. We extend the framework to multiple black boxes, each on its own input space, and show that aggregation can reduce prediction error relative to the best single black box. Simulated and real data demonstrate the practical value of the method.

</details>


### [200] [SSPO: Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12443)
*Yuanyu Li,Jintao Xu,Zijiang Liu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Jianshen Zhang,Wei Qi,Chen Xie,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出SSPO（结构感知相似性加权偏好优化），通过不一致性加权的留一基线联合评分所有B个采样解，解决神经组合优化（NCO）训练中两个失败模式：梯度信号极化（偏好优化仅锚定最佳解）和基线冗余（均值基线均匀加权导致结构相似解冗余）。在TSP、EFL和JSP基准上取得一致改进，并在京东生产性设施选址系统中部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NCO并行采样训练方法未能充分利用协同采样的解组中蕴含的丰富信息：偏好优化方法锚定单一最优解，丢弃了其他候选解的细粒度质量与结构信号（梯度信号极化）；均值基线则对候选解均匀加权，结构近乎相同的解会淹没基线并推高梯度方差（基线冗余）。

Method: SSPO采用不一致性加权的留一基线对所有B个采样解进行联合评分，使结构上不同的候选解获得更高权重，通过在单一机制中同时解决上述两个失败模式。其基线利用编码器已有的节点表示构建零参数、问题自适应的解嵌入。

Result: 在TSP、EFL和JSP基准上相较以往的最优锚定和均匀加权基线取得一致改进；与均匀RLOO在TSP和EFL上的直接对比表明结构感知加权是改进的主要驱动力。SSPO训练的EFL策略已在京东生产设施选址系统中部署，验证了规模化使用的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 结构感知的相似性加权是提升NCO训练的关键因素，SSPO通过解嵌入捕捉解之间的结构差异并据此重新分配基线权重，在标准化基准和真实生产系统中均证明了其有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) relies on parallel solution sampling for training, yet existing methods fail to fully exploit the rich information latent in a co-sampled solution group. Preference-optimization methods anchor on the single best solution and discard fine-grained quality and structural signal from all other peers-a failure we term gradient signal polarization. Mean-based baselines instead weight peers uniformly, so structurally near-identical peers flood the baseline with redundant information and keep gradient variance high-a failure we term baseline redundancy. We propose SSPO (Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization), which scores all $B$ sampled solutions jointly through a dissimilarity-weighted leave-one-out baseline: structurally distinct peers receive higher weight, resolving both failures in a single mechanism. The baseline uses zero-parameter, problem-adaptive solution embeddings built from the encoder's existing node representations. Experiments on TSP, EFL, and JSP benchmarks show consistent gains over prior best-anchor and uniform-weight baselines. A direct comparison against uniform RLOO on TSP and EFL confirms that structure-aware weighting is the primary driver of improvement. The SSPO-trained EFL policy has been deployed in a production facility-location system at JD$\mathord{.}$com, confirming practical viability at scale.

</details>


### [201] [Online Inference for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12973)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布强化学习中分位数时序差分学习（QTD）的统计推断问题。在生成模型假设下，建立了同步和异步QTD的函数中心极限定理，并提出了基于随机缩放的在线推断方法，构建渐近枢轴统计量，无需存储完整QTD迭代轨迹即可高效进行统计推断，大幅降低内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习中QTD算法的统计推断问题尚未得到充分研究。现有的推断方法通常需要存储完整的迭代轨迹，内存开销大且效率低，缺乏面向QTD的在线统计推断工具。

Method: 在生成模型可访问的假设下，首先为同步和异步QTD建立函数中心极限定理，证明QTD的平均迭代弱收敛到重标度布朗运动。然后基于随机缩放方法构造渐近枢轴统计量，利用QTD整条路径的信息进行推断，且该统计量可在线计算，无需存储全部迭代轨迹。

Result: 建立了同步与异步QTD的函数中心极限定理，证明了平均迭代对重标度布朗运动的弱收敛性；提出了基于随机缩放的在线推断方法，构造了渐近枢轴统计量，在实际显著减少了内存需求的同时实现了有效的统计推断。

Conclusion: 该工作为分布强化学习中的QTD算法提供了严格的理论基础的统计推断框架，所提出的在线推断方法在保证统计有效性的同时显著降低了内存开销，为分布强化学习的实际应用提供了高效的工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how to perform statistical inference for quantile temporal difference learning (QTD) in distributional reinforcement learning. Assuming access to a generative model, we first establish functional central limit theorems for both synchronous and asynchronous QTD, which show that the averaged iterates of QTD converge weakly to a rescaled Brownian motion. We next provide online inference methods. Based on random scaling, the inference procedure constructs an asymptotically pivotal statistic for inference by using the information along the whole QTD path. Meanwhile, the proposed statistic can be computed online without storing the entire trajectory of QTD iterates. This substantially reduces the memory requirement and enables efficient statistical inference in distributional reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [202] [Statistical Properties of Robust Learning under Distributional Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13133)
*Zhiyi Li,Xiaojie Mao,Yunbei Xu,Ruohan Zhan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布偏移下两种鲁棒学习框架（DRO和RS）在目标环境中的有限样本泛化误差界，提出了基于部分偏移信息的信息导向超参数校准方法，并在网络批量采购问题中进行了应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRO和RS的有限样本分析通常只保证源环境或最坏情况下的性能，缺乏对目标环境下（偏移后分布）泛化误差的系统性刻画与两种方法的比较。该文旨在填补这一理论空白，为目标环境下的泛化误差提供明确的有限样本界。

Method: 首先为DRO和RS推导目标偏移环境下的有限样本泛化误差界，显式刻画鲁棒超参数带来的偏移敏感度降低与正则化惩罚之间的权衡，并避免Wasserstein经验集中的维度灾难。其次，在获得部分偏移信息（如偏移幅度或方向）时，提出信息导向的超参数校准方法，并在相同信息下比较两种方法。最后将框架应用于网络批量采购问题。

Result: 推导出DRO和RS在目标偏移环境下的有限样本泛化误差界，该界显式展示了偏移敏感度与正则化惩罚间的权衡且避免了维度灾难。在部分信息机制下，DRO和RS表现出互补的理论与实证行为。网络批量采购问题的应用解释了鲁棒策略如何应对需求分布的正向偏移。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了鲁棒学习方法在分布偏移下统计性质理解的空白，为DRO和RS的系统性比较提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Distributional shifts arise when the target deployment environment differs from the source environment that generated the training data. Robust learning frameworks such as Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO) and Robust Satisficing (RS) aim to address this challenge, yet their finite-sample guarantees under such shifts, and their systematic comparison, remain underexplored: existing analyses typically establish guarantees either in the source environment or for adversarial worst-case performance over an ambiguity set. This paper instead studies generalization error in the target environment---the excess loss under the shifted target distribution. Our contributions are threefold. First, we derive finite-sample generalization error bounds in the shifted target environment for both DRO and RS. These bounds explicitly characterize the trade-off between reduced sensitivity to shift and the regularization penalty induced by each method's robustness hyperparameter, and they avoid the curse of dimensionality associated with Wasserstein empirical concentration. Second, when partial shift information such as shift magnitude or direction is available, we propose information-directed hyperparameter calibrations and compare the two methods given the same information. Under these calibrations, and in the partial-information regimes we study, DRO and RS exhibit complementary theoretical and empirical behavior. Finally, we apply the framework to a network lot-sizing problem, using it to interpret how robust policies respond to positive shifts in the demand distribution. Together, these results fill a gap in understanding the statistical properties of robust learning methods under distributional shifts and provide a principled basis for comparing DRO and RS.

</details>


### [203] [High-dimensional networks and mean squared error for possibly misspecified models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13171)
*Lourens Waldorp*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究高维网络分析中的邻居选择问题。在线性模型设定下，岭参数的引入导致低测试方差和大量虚假边；在非线性设定下则需要惩罚。本文证明在高维场景中，最小描述长度（MDL）能产生保守的（低假阳性率）邻居估计，优于Lasso、AIC、BIC等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络分析中越来越多变量被纳入，以避免遗漏重要变量及其连接。在高维场景（参数远多于观测）下，传统邻居选择方法（Lasso、AIC、BIC）容易产生大量虚假边（高假阳性率），需要一种既能正确选择邻居又能控制假阳性率的方法。

Method: 作者在线性模型框架下分析邻居选择问题，考察岭参数对均方误差的影响及其导致的低测试方差和大量边；并将此与机器学习中的双下降现象联系起来。随后证明在高维设定下，采用最小描述长度（MDL）准则，将模型空间的体积纳入罚项，可以在模型正确或错误假设为线性的两种情况下均获得正确或更小的邻居集合（低假阳性率）。

Result: （1）高维网络中，传统惩罚方法（Lasso、AIC、BIC）会产生大量虚假边；（2）岭参数会降低测试方差但导致邻居边数膨胀；（3）在高维设定下，MDL准则能保证正确的邻居选择或产生更小的邻居集合，即具有低假阳性率的保守估计，无论真实模型是线性还是非线性。

Conclusion: 高维网络邻居选择需要将模型空间的体积纳入罚项。MDL方法相比传统方法能提供保守且准确的邻居估计，有效控制假阳性率，该发现将网络分析与传统统计模型选择及机器学习双下降理论联系起来。

Abstract: To avoid missing important variables and their connections in networks, more and more variables are included in network analysis. Here we show that in a setting with many more parameters than observations (high-dimensional) it is possible to get a conservative (i.e., low false positive rate) estimate of the neighbourhood for each node (which connections are in the network). A neighbourhood is often estimated with a linear model, and this leads to two interesting cases: (i) If the true model is linear, then neighbourhood selection work reasonably well, and (ii) if the true model is nonlinear, then neighbourhood selection requires a penalty for the high dimensions. Here we show the impact of the ridge parameter on the mean squared error, and how this leads to low test variance and hence to neighbourhoods with large numbers of edges. We connect these insights with results from machine learning, where the so-called double descent (when more parameters are included than observations, the mean squared error goes down a second time) has put the traditional view on model selection upside down. Essentially, for adequate neighbourhood selection in models with a large number of parameters, the volume of the model space needs to be included in the penalty. Most neighbourhood selection methods (e.g., Lasso, AIC, BIC) lead to spurious edges (high false positive rate), but we prove that in the high-dimensional setting, minimum description length leads to correct neighbourhood selection or smaller (low false positive rates) in both cases when either the model is correctly or incorrectly assumed linear

</details>


### [204] [Sinkhorn Linearization and the Spectral Proxy: Unifying the Statistical and Algorithmic Theory of Feature-Parameterized Inverse Optimal Transport via a Single Spectral Sandwich](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13201)
*Han Dong,Jiaming Li,Yongqiang Gong,Ruixi Li,Yin Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了特征参数化代价函数下逆最优传输（IOT）的统计与算法理论。核心贡献是Sinkhorn线性化（熵化OT方案对代价的隐函数敏感性）及其谱代理公式。基于谱三明治界，作者证明了四个定理（可辨识性、稀疏一致性、适定性和收敛性）和一个观察（错设定），为IOT问题的可辨识、稀疏恢复、适定性和算法收敛提供了完整且严格的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优传输（IOT）是估计代价函数参数θ的关键问题，但其统计与算法理论尚不完整。本文旨在通过特征参数化代价C_θ的框架，系统性地建立IOT的可辨识性、稀疏性恢复、问题适定性以及优化收敛性的严格理论基础。

Method: 提出Sinkhorn线性化——熵化OT方案对代价的隐函数灵敏度分析，并给出谱精确且几何透明的谱代理公式。通过受限Hessian的谱三明治界（(π_min/ε)I ≤ H_T^{-1} ≤ (π_max/ε)I）推导核心界σ_min ≥ (π_min/(a_max ε))√(λ_min(Σ))。基于此核心分别建立T1（商空间上的全局单射可辨识性）、T2（l1惩罚估计器的稀疏一致性）、T3（特征矩映射的强单调性与Lipschitz逆）、T4（梯度下降的局部强凸收敛）以及O5（错设定下的投影收敛与Holder连续性数值评估）。

Result: T1：θ在规范核商空间上全局单射，维度界为F ≤ (K-1)²；T2：l1惩罚估计器在不可表示性和得分集中条件下以指数失败概率恢复真实支撑集；T3：特征矩映射M(θ)强单调，逆映射Lipschitz常数为L ≤ ε||Φ^T S_a||_op/(π_min λ_min(Σ))；T4：在μ ≥ π_min² λ_min(Σ)/ε²下保证单调梯度下降收敛；O5：错设定下估计器收敛到OT模型投影，投影映射的Holder连续性经验指数α_eff ∈ (0,1)。

Conclusion: 本文通过Sinkhorn线性化和谱代理公式，为特征参数化IOT问题提供了统一且完备的理论框架，涵盖了可辨识性、稀疏恢复、适定性和优化收敛四个核心方面，并拓展到错设定情形。这些界都清晰地依赖于代价熵正则系数ε、计划边缘概率以及特征协方差的谱性质，为实际IOT估计器的设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We develop the statistical and algorithmic theory of inverse optimal transport (IOT) under the feature-parameterized cost C_theta(i,j) = -theta^T phi(i,j). The core technical contribution is the Sinkhorn linearization -- the implicit-function sensitivity of the entropic OT plan to the cost -- together with its spectral proxy, a formula that is spectrally exact yet geometrically transparent.
  The restricted Hessian on the tangent space satisfies the spectral sandwich (pi_min/epsilon) I <= H_T^{-1} <= (pi_max/epsilon) I, yielding the single core bound sigma_min >= (pi_min/(a_max epsilon)) sqrt(lambda_min(Sigma)) that drives the entire theory. On this core we establish four theorems and one observation.
  T1 (identifiability): theta is globally injective on the quotient of the gauge kernel, with dimension bound F <= (K-1)^2. T2 (sparsistency): the l1-penalized estimator recovers the true support under irrepresentability and score concentration, with exponential failure probability. T3 (well-posedness): the feature-moment map M(theta) = Phi^T x_theta is strongly monotone, and the inverse is Lipschitz with constant L <= epsilon ||Phi^T S_a||_op / (pi_min lambda_min(Sigma)). T4 (convergence): local strong convexity with mu >= pi_min^2 lambda_min(Sigma) / epsilon^2 guarantees monotone gradient descent convergence. O5 (misspecification): the estimator converges to the OT-model projection of the truth; the Holder continuity of the projection map is assessed numerically, yielding setting-dependent empirical exponents alpha_eff in (0,1).

</details>


### [205] [Wasserstein Filtering: A Sample Selection Method for Robust Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13418)
*Yikai Xu,Zhao Chen,Jian Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Wasserstein Filtering (WF) 的样本筛选框架，通过丢弃可疑样本并用剩余数据的经验分布估计目标分布，从而从受污染的混合数据中恢复底层清洁分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中的数据常含有部分污染样本，如何从受污染数据中恢复底层真实分布是一个核心问题。现有方法缺乏既能分离远距离异常又能处理局部不可区分扰动的统一框架，因此需要一种模型无关的、理论可保证的预处理工具。

Method: WF 的核心思想是选择使经验分布到完全污染经验分布的 Wasserstein 距离最大化的样本子集，从而优先隔离并移除具有几何影响力的离群点。为实现可计算性，作者提出三种算法：边际筛选方案 SinkMarg（基于熵最优传输）、联合优化算法 SinkWF 和 SlicedWF（分别利用熵最优传输和切片 Wasserstein 近似）。此外，提出了 FELP（Far Exclusion and Local Projection）污染模型，用于刻画由可分离离群点和局部不可区分扰动组成的污染结构。

Result: 在理论上，作者证明在 FELP 污染模型下，WF 估计器在具有有界协方差的分布族上达到极小极大最优性。数值实验涵盖合成数据集、基准异常检测套件以及基于扩散模型的鲁棒生成学习，结果表明 WF 是一种实用且模型无关的预处理工具，在异常检测上表现竞争性，并在重度污染条件下为生成建模带来显著下游收益。

Conclusion: WF 作为一种通用的样本筛选框架，兼具理论最优性和实际可用性，既可作为异常检测的竞争性方法，也可作为生成建模等下游任务的鲁棒预处理工具。

Abstract: Given a dataset where a portion of the samples are contaminated, our goal is to recover the underlying clean population distribution. To this end, we propose Wasserstein Filtering (WF), a novel sample selection framework that discards a fraction of suspicious samples and estimates the target distribution using the empirical measure of the remaining data. The core insight is to select a subset of samples whose empirical distribution maximizes its Wasserstein distance to the fully contaminated empirical distribution, thereby preferentially isolating and removing geometrically influential outliers. To render this optimization computationally tractable, we introduce three algorithms: a marginal screening scheme, SinkMarg, and two joint optimization algorithms, SinkWF and SlicedWF, leveraging entropic optimal transport and sliced Wasserstein approximations, respectively. On the theoretical front, we introduce the Far Exclusion and Local Projection (FELP) contamination model, which characterizes corruptions consisting of well-separated outliers and locally indistinguishable perturbations. Under this model, we prove that the WF estimator achieves minimax optimality over distribution families with bounded covariance. Extensive numerical experiments on synthetic datasets, benchmark anomaly detection suites, and robust generative learning with diffusion models demonstrate that WF serves as a highly practical, model-agnostic preprocessing tool. It delivers competitive outlier detection performance and provides substantial downstream benefits for generative modeling under heavy contamination.

</details>


### [206] [Bagging Robustly Learns VC Classes with Linear Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13514)
*Omar Montasser*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了对抗鲁棒学习问题，证明VC类可以用与VC维d线性相关的样本复杂度进行对抗鲁棒学习，相比之前上界实现了指数级改进。算法结合了bagging与鲁棒经验风险最小化(RERM)，并对样本下界给出匹配证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗样本测试时的鲁棒学习是机器学习的重要问题。Montasser等(2019)给出的样本复杂度上界过于宽松，本文旨在证明更紧的上界，并寻求简单有效的实际算法。

Method: 提出一种简单的非正确(improper)学习算法：在O(d^*)个独立的bootstrap采样上分别计算RERM，然后对这些RERM预测结果取多数表决（majority vote），其中d^*为对偶VC维。并从理论上证明了该方法的样本复杂度为线性于VC维d。

Result: 证明了VC类可以以线性于VC维d的样本复杂度实现对抗鲁棒学习，比之前的指数级上界有本质改进。同时给出了下界：任何学习者在oracle模型下至少需要Ω(d^*)次RERM oracle调用，证明该算法在此意义上不可改进。

Conclusion: Bagging与RERM的组合在对抗鲁棒学习中被证明兼具理论最优性与实际简洁性，上界与下界匹配，彻底回答了该oracle模型下RERM调用次数的复杂度问题。

Abstract: We revisit the problem of learning predictors robust to adversarial examples at test-time. We prove that VC classes are adversarially robustly learnable with sample complexity linear in the VC dimension $d$, providing an exponential improvement over the previous upper bound of Montasser, Hanneke, and Srebro (2019). Remarkably, this result is achieved with a simple improper algorithm that combines the classic heuristic bagging (bootstrap aggregation) of Breiman (1996) with robust empirical risk minimization (RERM). Our algorithm computes RERMs on $O(d^\star)$ independent bootstrap samples and outputs their majority vote, where $d^\star$ denotes the dual VC dimension. We complement this result with a lower bound showing that this is unavoidable: in general, any learner in this oracle model requires $Ω(d^\star)$ calls to an RERM oracle, even when given arbitrarily many training examples.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [Density Estimation on Compact Manifolds under Intrinsic Spectral Block Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12637)
*Olga Klopp,Fedor Noskov*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在紧致连通黎曼流形上进行非参数密度估计的固有谱稀疏模型。不同于在任意选取的Laplace-Beltrami特征基中惩罚系数，该方法对每个完整特征空间分组并度量其Hilbert范数，从而构建出基无关、等距不变的块变分空间。文章建立了该空间的结构、原子和非线性逼近性质，阐明了其与Sobolev、Besov及逐系数谱ℓ¹类的关系，并构造了无坐标的块收缩估计器，证明了非渐近的、信号依赖的L²-oracle不等式。在多项式谱增长下，风险理论将谱块数量与重数分离，呈现两种机制；在匹配的谱增长和非退化假设下，相应的极小极大下界表明这种重数依赖是内在的，对球面和SO(3)旋转群有显著后果。此外，文章还发展了用于对数密度的正的、归一化的块惩罚指数谱筛，并推导了似然oracle不等式以及期望Kullback-Leibler、Hellinger和L²风险界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏密度估计方法在流形上依赖于任意选取的Laplace-Beltrami特征基，导致估计结果对基的选择敏感。作者旨在构建一种不依赖特定特征基、具有几何不变性的稀疏密度估计理论框架，以更自然地适应流形的内在结构。

Method: 该工作将每个完整的Laplace-Beltrami特征空间视为一个块，用其Hilbert范数度量稀疏性，构建块变分空间。基于此提出无坐标的块收缩估计器，并在多项式谱增长假设下分析其风险。同时开发了块惩罚指数谱筛（block-penalized exponential spectral sieve）用于对数密度估计，推导似然oracle不等式。

Result: 建立了块变分空间的结构、原子和非线性逼近性质及其与Sobolev、Besov和系数wise谱ℓ¹类的关系。证明了非渐近信号依赖的L²-oracle不等式，揭示风险理论在多项式谱增长下呈现两种机制：单一高维特征空间驱动和累积谱复杂度驱动。相应极小极大下界表明重数依赖是内在的，对球面和SO(3)给出了尖锐结论；并推导了对数密度估计的期望KL、Hellinger和L²风险界。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种尊重流形几何结构的稀疏密度估计理论，其估计器在特征基选择下保持不变（等距不变），并将谱块重数纳入极小极大风险理论的核心考量，标志着流形上稀疏密度估计理论的基无关化进展。

Abstract: We introduce an intrinsic spectral sparsity model for nonparametric density estimation on compact connected Riemannian manifolds. Instead of penalizing coefficients in an arbitrarily chosen Laplace--Beltrami eigenbasis, we group each complete eigenspace and measure the Hilbert norm of its spectral component. The resulting block-variation space is basis independent and isometry invariant. We establish its structural, atomic, and nonlinear approximation properties and clarify its relation to Sobolev, Besov, and coefficientwise spectral $\ell^1$ classes. We then construct a coordinate-free block-shrinkage estimator and prove a nonasymptotic signal-dependent $L^2$-oracle inequality that adapts to the unknown set of detectable eigenspaces. Under polynomial spectral growth, the risk theory separates the number of spectral blocks from their multiplicities and exhibits two regimes: one driven by a single high-dimensional eigenspace and the other by cumulative spectral complexity. Under matching spectral-growth and nondegeneracy assumptions, corresponding minimax lower bounds show that this multiplicity dependence is intrinsic, with sharp consequences for spheres and the rotation group $SO(3)$. Finally, we develop a positive, normalized, block-penalized exponential spectral sieve for log-densities and derive likelihood oracle inequalities together with expected Kullback--Leibler, Hellinger, and $L^2$ risk bounds. The resulting framework provides a geometry-respecting theory of sparse density estimation that remains invariant under changes of eigenbasis.

</details>


### [208] [Universality of e-detectors for ARL control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12660)
*Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了变更检测中"e检测器"（e-detector）的性质。e检测器不仅控制平均运行长度（ARL），还满足"可选视野不等式"（optional-horizon inequality），即P(T≤σ)≤E[σ]/b对所有数据相关的停时σ成立，从而避免早期误报。更重要的结论是反方向也成立：任何满足可选视野不等式的停时T必然来自某个e检测器的阈值化。此外，仅满足ARL控制的停时可由"弱e检测器"表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上e检测器主要用于控制ARL，但本文旨在揭示e检测器具有更强的统计性质——可选视野不等式，它保证在任意自适应监测窗口下都不易发生早期误报。同时，作者希望建立这些性质的完整性刻画：即这些性质不仅由e检测器充分保证，而且是其充分必要条件。

Method: 作者首先形式化定义了e检测器（非负过程M，满足E_P[M_τ]≤E_P[τ]对所有停时τ和P∈P成立），然后证明阈值化e检测器得到的停时T_b满足可选视野不等式。关键贡献是证明了逆定理：任意满足可选视野不等式的停时必然等价于某个e检测器的阈值化结果。此外，通过放宽条件至仅要求阈值停时上的约束，定义了"弱e检测器"，并证明了ARL控制与弱e检测器之间的等价表示定理。附录中还给出了其他变更检测指标的普适表示。

Result: (1) e检测器阈值化所得到的停时满足可选视野不等式，且在全概率类P上保持ARL≥b；(2) 可选视野不等式的逆命题成立——任何满足该不等式的停时都可以由e检测器阈值化表示；(3) 仅满足ARL控制的停时可由弱e检测器普适表示，其中弱e检测器仅在阈值停时T_b上要求E_P[M_τ]≤E_P[τ]成立。

Conclusion: e检测器不仅是控制ARL的工具，其本质特征在于对应的停时满足可选视野不等式，这为变更检测程序提供了更强的保证（避免早期误报）。该性质与e检测器之间的等价性表明e检测器在变更检测理论中具有基础性地位。弱e检测器的引入则为仅关注ARL的场景提供了更宽松但依然完备的表示框架。

Abstract: An e-detector for a pre-change class $\mathcal P$ is a nonnegative process $M$ such that $\mathbb E_P[M_τ] \leq \mathbb E_P[τ]$ for all stopping times $τ$ and all $P \in \mathcal P$. Thresholding e-detectors controls the average run length (ARL): declaring a change at the first time $T_b$ when $M$ crosses $b$ ensures that $\inf_{P \in \mathcal P}\mathbb E_P[T] \geq b$. But e-detectors do substantially more than control the ARL; they also satisfy a \emph{optional-horizon inequality}: \[ P(T_b\leqσ)\leq \mathbb E_P[σ]/b \] for every data-dependent stopping time (monitoring horizon) \(σ\) and $P\in \mathcal P$. In particular, every e-detector-based procedure obeys $P(T\leq t)\leq t/b$ at each fixed $t$, thus avoiding early false alarms. Remarkably, the converse also holds: every stopping time $T$ that satisfies the optional-horizon inequality must in fact arise from thresholding an e-detector. We also derive a universal representation of stopping times that satisfy (only) ARL control. These are represented by \emph{weak} e-detectors, that only require $\mathbb E_P[M_τ] \leq \mathbb E_P[τ]$ to hold at all threshold stopping times $T_b$. Appendices present universal representations for other (less common) change detection metrics.

</details>


### [209] [Sharp proper estimation of fixed-component Gaussian location mixtures in polynomial time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12701)
*Hengzhi He,Guang Cheng*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文解决了Doss等人在2023年提出的开放问题：在无分离条件、无最小权重假设下，对至多k个单位协方差高斯混合（均值在固定半径球内），在多项式时间内构造达到最优Hellinger风险速率√(d/n)∧1的估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Doss等人(2023)证明了单位协方差高斯混合的最小最大Hellinger风险为√(d/n)∧1，但仅构造了一个速率为(d/n)^{1/4}的多项式时间估计器。对于固定k≥3，在多项式时间内达到最优速率一直是悬而未决的问题。

Method: 核心装置是"矩纤维范围查找器"(moment-fiber range finder)。首先利用二阶矩子空间控制投影丢失的能量；然后估计有限个"单自由指标"的Hermite收缩（向量值收缩），这些收缩能以√(d/n)的精确尺度恢复恰好包含一个丢失方向的所有张量分量；剩余项至少包含两个丢失因子，由残差二阶矩能量控制。最终得到的子空间维度仅依赖于k，在此常数维空间中做穷举矩拟合构造合适的高斯混合。

Result: 获得了与最小最大风险相匹配的最优Hellinger速率√(d/n)∧1，并且在任意固定k的多项式算术时间内可实现，彻底解决了该开放问题。

Conclusion: 结合无维数依赖的矩特征化，穷举矩拟合与常数维子空间方法共同实现了高斯混合估计在计算复杂度与统计最优性之间的完全统一，证明了统计最优达成的同时计算并不需要额外代价。

Abstract: We consider a mixture of at most $k$ unit-covariance Gaussians in $\mathbb{R}^d$ whose means belong to a fixed-radius ball, with no separation or minimum-weight condition. Doss, Wu, Yang and Zhou (2023) proved that the minimax Hellinger risk is of order $\sqrt{d/n}\wedge 1$ and constructed a proper polynomial-time estimator with the slower general bound $(d/n)^{1/4}$; obtaining the sharp rate in polynomial time for fixed $k\geq 3$ was left open. We resolve this question. The key device is a moment-fiber range finder. A second-moment subspace controls the energy missed by projection. We then estimate finitely many one-free-index Hermite contractions. These vector-valued contractions recover every tensor component containing exactly one missed direction at the sharp $\sqrt{d/n}$ scale. Every remaining term contains at least two missed factors and is therefore controlled by the residual second-moment energy. The resulting subspace has dimension depending only on $k$. Exhaustive moment fitting in this constant-dimensional space produces a proper mixture and, together with the dimension-free moment characterization of Gaussian mixtures, achieves the optimal Hellinger rate in polynomial arithmetic time for every fixed $k$.

</details>


### [210] [Hyper-V uniform ergodicity of Markov chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12738)
*Austin Brown,Kshitij Khare*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的均匀漂移条件和局部小化条件，推导出比标准均匀遍历性更强的加权形式——超V均匀遍历性，并通过Pólya-Gamma和Kolmogorov-Gamma Gibbs采样器验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的全局小化条件在实践中往往难以验证，而现有的均匀遍历性框架又需要较强的假设。作者希望绕过全局小化条件，同时获得比全局小化更强的收敛保证。

Method: 提出新的均匀漂移条件与局部小化条件，推导出称为"超V均匀遍历性"(hyper-V uniform ergodicity)的加权均匀遍历性。该框架不依赖全局小化条件，建立了极小极大意义下的最优收敛界，并通过两变量Gibbs采样的不变测度形式直接推断超V均匀遍历性收敛。

Result: 建立了超V均匀遍历性的收敛保证，实现了对受控函数V控制下的所有函数与初始化无关的几何偏差衰减；证明了框架的收敛界在极小极大意义下最优；成功应用于Pólya-Gamma和Kolmogorov-Gamma Gibbs采样器，并展示了从不变测度形式直接推断收敛性的方法。

Conclusion: 该框架通过局部小化替代全局小化条件，简化了验证难度同时增强了收敛保证，为Markov链的均匀遍历性分析提供了更实用的工具，特别是在Gibbs采样器的收敛性分析中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We develop a new uniform drift condition and local minorization that implies a stronger weighted form of uniform ergodicity for Markov chains we call hyper-V uniform ergodicity. The convergence guarantees geometric decay of the bias towards the invariant measure independently of the initialization for all functions controlled by a dominating function V. A key advantage of the approach is that it bypasses the need to establish a global minorization condition, which is often substantially more difficult to verify in practice, while yielding stronger convergence guarantees than global minorization. Optimal convergence bounds in a minimax sense of the framework are established. The utility of the framework is demonstrated through applications to the P'olya-Gamma and Kolmogorov-Gamma Gibbs samplers. We also show qualitative hyper-V uniform ergodicity convergence for two-variable Gibbs samplers can be inferred by the form of the invariant measure, bypassing convergence analysis entirely.

</details>


### [211] [Weighted Nuclear Elastic Net Estimation of (Near-) Low-Rank Drift Matrices in Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12838)
*Dmytro Marushkevych,Francisco Pina,Mark Podolskij*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究连续观测的高维 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程中漂移矩阵的估计问题，针对漂移矩阵精确或近似低秩的情况，提出了一种加权核弹性网估计器（Weighted Nuclear Elastic Net Estimator），结合岭正则化与核范数惩罚，并建立了普适对角化谱框架下的 oracle 不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程中精确低秩漂移会引入非稳定方向，导致非遍历机制，使得经验协方差矩阵病态化。这给漂移矩阵的估计带来了本质性困难，需要设计新的估计方法以克服协方差矩阵条件数不佳的问题。

Method: 提出加权核弹性网估计器，同时结合岭正则化（ridge regularization）处理病态协方差矩阵，以及核范数惩罚（nuclear-norm penalty）促进低秩结构，且惩罚项表达在经验似然几何（empirical likelihood geometry）中。理论分析采用一般对角化谱框架建立 oracle 不等式，随机项通过自归一化鞅论证控制。

Result: 建立了针对任意低秩比较矩阵的 oracle 不等式；对于近低秩漂移，近似误差通过加权正则化经验协方差后的奇异值衰减自然度量。在对称正半定精确低秩模型下，验证了经验曲率条件，估计器的平方 Frobenius 误差达到 $O(rd\log(T)/T)$ 的高概率阶，即标准秩-$r$ 矩阵估计量级（含对数因子），且需满足显式的维度-时间窗口条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的加权核弹性网估计器在高维非遍历 OU 过程中有效解决了病态协方差问题，并实现了接近最优的低秩矩阵估计精度（$rd/T$ 量级），为低秩漂移估计提供了理论保障与可操作的调参准则。

Abstract: We study estimation of the drift matrix in a continuously observed high-dimensional Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process when the drift is exactly or approximately low rank. In this setting, exact low rank induces non-stable directions and hence a non-ergodic regime, resulting in a poorly conditioned empirical covariance matrix. To address this difficulty, we introduce a Weighted Nuclear Elastic Net Estimator that combines ridge regularization with a nuclear-norm penalty expressed in the empirical likelihood geometry.
  Under a general diagonalizable spectral framework, we establish oracle inequalities relative to arbitrary low-rank comparison matrices. For near low-rank drifts, the approximation error is naturally measured through the singular-value decay of the drift after weighting by the regularized empirical covariance. The stochastic term is controlled by self-normalized martingale arguments under appropriate choice of the tuning parameter.
  For a symmetric positive-semidefinite exact low-rank model, we verify the empirical-curvature condition required to translate the weighted bound into a Frobenius-norm bound. With an appropriate choice of tuning parameters, the resulting estimator satisfies, up to a logarithmic factor, the standard rank-$r$ matrix-estimation scaling $r d/T$: specifically, its squared Frobenius error is of order $r d\log(T)/T$ with high probability, under an explicit dimension-horizon condition.

</details>


### [212] [Foundations of Independent Component Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13229)
*Patrick Forré*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文以自包含笔记的形式,面向具有测度论概率论背景的读者,系统阐述了线性独立成分分析(ICA)模型的数学基础。文章首先发展了R^d上概率测度特征函数的理论,包括其解析性及对分布的刻画作用;随后在不同强度的源假设下(从非常数、非高斯到无高斯成分)给出了ICA模型的若干可识别性结果,证明了在最严格假设下独立源可在平移、置换、尺度和符号意义上被识别(即使存在加性高斯噪声);最后介绍了用于从数据中恢复独立源的在线等变梯度下降ICA算法(适用于标准完整无噪声非高斯ICA场景)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICA模型在盲源分离等领域广泛应用,但其数学基础(f尤其是可识别性条件)在文献中往往分散且缺乏系统性整理,且部分论述需要较强的测度论基础。作者旨在提供一份自包含的数学笔记,使具备概率测度论背景的读者能够完整理解ICA模型的理论基础、可识别性条件及其证明。

Method: 本文采用公理化推导与定理证明的方法:首先系统构建R^d上概率测度特征函数理论(解析性与分布决定特性);其次在源的假设逐级强化(非常数→非高斯→无高斯成分)的框架下,依次证明ICA模型的可识别性结论;最后给出在线等变梯度下降算法,用于在标准完整无噪非高斯ICA设置下恢复独立源。

Result: 在源为非高斯的无高斯成分(最严格)假设下,证明了独立源在平移、置换、尺度和符号意义上可识别,且该结果在存在加性高斯噪声时仍然成立;并给出了可在数据上实际运行的在线等变梯度下降ICA算法。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个完整、自洽的ICA数学基础框架,明确了不同强度假设下的可识别性边界,并提供了对应场景下的实用恢复算法,兼具理论深度与算法实用性。

Abstract: We present the mathematical foundations of linear independent component analysis (ICA) models based on standard literature in a self-contained note. It is aimed at readers with a background in measure-theoretic probability theory. We first develop the theory of the characteristic functions of probability measures on $\mathbb{R}^d$, including their analyticity and the way in which they determine and characterise the distributions. We then focus on several identifiability results of ICA models with successively strengthened assumptions on the sources: from merely non-constant, to non-Gaussian, to Gaussian-free independent sources. Under the strictest assumptions, we show that the independent sources are identifiable up to translation, permutation, scales and signs, and this even in the presence of additive Gaussian noise. Furthermore, we present the online equivariant gradient descent ICA algorithm for recovering the independent sources from data, in the standard complete noiseless non-Gaussian ICA setting.

</details>


### [213] [On Bridging Mixture Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13383)
*Pierre Del Moral,Ajay Jasra,Ke Zhao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在两个混合概率测度之间建立"桥接"。作者提出了一种基于Markov核的通用机制，可从一种混合分布生成样本过渡到另一种混合分布，并证明了该方法的熵最优性。针对高斯混合和Schrödinger桥的情形，作者证明了精确桥与基于ε-协方差膨胀的近似桥之间的2-Wasserstein连续性界，该界依赖于对扰动Riccati映射的连续性分析。作者给出了三种情形（高斯混合、单一高斯、经验估计）下的收敛速率，并进行了数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合概率测度之间的桥接在许多应用中具有重要价值（如最优传输、生成建模、粒子滤波等），但现有的桥接方法往往缺乏理论保证，且在实际中精确的混合成分和Markov核通常不可获得。本文旨在提供一种通用且具有理论最优性保证的混合桥接框架，并分析在参数估计误差下的鲁棒性。

Method: 作者提出基于给定参考和扩展状态空间的通用桥接机制，证明其熵最优性。针对实际场景，考虑高斯混合和Schrödinger桥，通过ε-协方差膨胀对桥进行近似。关键的技术贡献在于对扰动Riccati映射的连续性分析，从而导出精确桥与近似桥之间2-Wasserstein距离的理论界。对于高斯混合参数用EM算法估计的情形，给出高概率上界；对于单一高斯和经验估计Monge映射的情形，给出期望意义下的界。

Result: 对于高斯混合（EM参数估计）情形，在假设条件下以至少1-10N^{-1}的概率，精确桥与近似桥之间的2-Wasserstein距离上界为O([(d log N/N)^{1/2}{1+(d log N/N)^{1/2}(ε^{-2}+1)}+ε^2])；对于单一高斯和经验估计Monge映射两种情形，期望界的量级为O(d{(1+ε^{-2})/(1+N)+ε^2})。数值实验验证了理论界的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了混合概率测度间桥接的通用理论框架，证明了其熵最优性，并在高斯混合和Schrödinger桥的具体场景下导出了系统化的收敛速率分析。结果表明，随着样本量N增加和协方差膨胀参数ε趋近于0，近似桥以2-Wasserstein度量收敛到精确桥。该工作为混合分布间的最优传输桥接提供了坚实的理论基础和可操作的误差控制手段。

Abstract: In this article we consider bridging between two mixture probability measures. In particular, given access to a Markov kernel between two component distributions, we provide a general mechanism to generate samples from one mixture to the other. Associated to a given reference and extended state space, we prove entropic optimality of this approach. In order to use this idea one needs to know the underlying mixtures and the Markov kernel, which is seldom available, and so we consider the case of Gaussian mixtures and Schrödinger Bridges. We prove a general $2-$Wasserstein continuity bound between the exact bridge and one that is approximated, based on $ε-$covariance inflation, and these rely on a novel continuity analysis of perturbed Riccati maps. We apply our results in the context of bridging mixtures of Gaussians, single Gaussians and empirical estimators of the Gaussian parameters and the Monge map. For mixtures of Gaussians, when the parameters are estimated using the Expectation-Maxmization algorithm, the upper-bound on the $2-$Wasserstein distance between the true and approximated bridges is, under assumptions and with probability at least $1-10N^{-1}$, $\mathcal{O}\big(\big[\big(\tfrac{d\log N}{N}\big)^{1/2}\left\{1+\big(\tfrac{d\log N}{N}\right)^{1/2}(ε^{-2}+1)\big\}+ε^2\big]\big) $
and for the other two cases, in expectation, $\mathcal{O}\left(d\left\{\tfrac{1+ε^{-2}}{1+N}+ε^2\right\}\right)$, where $d,N\in\mathbb{N}$ is the dimension of the Gaussian and the number of empirical samples respectively. We also investigate our bounds numerically.

</details>


### [214] [On the Structural Limits of Machine Learning Decision Systems: An Information-Theoretic, Interaction-Based, and Stochastic-Dynamical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13510)
*Nestor R. Barraza,Gabriel Pena*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文从信息论和交互建模的角度系统审视了数据驱动决策系统的内在极限：通过Fano界和Cramér-Rao不等式阐述分类与参数估计的不可逾越的性能边界，指出这些界限由底层数据生成模型决定而非算法复杂度；进而讨论独立性、遍历性和分布稳定性等隐式假设对推断有效性的影响，并基于马尔可夫随机场和势函数表示编码依赖机制，最后将LLM集成智能体系统视为反馈驱动的随机过程，强调充分的数据模型是扩展预测能力的先决条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习方法的评估通常聚焦于预测精度和计算效率，但忽略了其性能本质上受限于数据生成过程的结构属性。作者旨在从信息论和交互建模的双重视角揭示这些结构性约束，纠正对算法能力过度乐观的看法，强调充分的数据建模是突破预测能力瓶颈的前提。

Method: 采用理论分析的方法：利用Fano不等式推导分类任务的最小可达到误差下界，利用Cramér-Rao不等式刻画参数估计的精度极限；系统梳理隐式假设（独立性、遍历性、分布稳定性）对推断有效性的影响；基于交互建模原理，回顾马尔可夫随机场和势函数表示等依赖编码框架；将LLM集成智能体架构建模为状态依赖的反馈驱动随机过程，以解释可能的涌现宏观行为。

Result: 揭示了分类和参数估计中存在与算法选择无关的信息论下界；明确了隐式统计假设的违背会导致推断失效；展示了马尔可夫随机场等交互模型能够编码复杂依赖结构；论证了LLM集成智能体系统作为反馈随机过程可产生涌现行为，从而印证了数据模型充分性对预测能力的关键约束作用。

Conclusion: 算法学习的性能被信息论界限所框定，改进模型对数据的刻画能力而非单纯优化算法，才是提升预测能力的核心路径；数据建模应被视为算法学习的前提条件，这一视角为理解和设计高性能数据驱动决策系统提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Machine learning procedures are commonly evaluated in terms of predictive accuracy and computational efficiency. However, their achievable performance is fundamentally constrained by structural properties of the underlying data-generating process, which are formalized in terms of informational bounds. In this work we examine intrinsic limits of data-driven decision systems from an information-theoretic and interaction-based perspective. We analyze minimal achievable error in classification through Fano-type bounds and precision limits in parametric estimation via the Cramér-Rao inequality, emphasizing that such limits depend on the underlying model rather than on algorithmic sophistication alone. We further discuss how implicit assumptions, such as independence, ergodicity, and distributional stability, affect the validity of inferential procedures. Building on interaction-based modeling principles, we review typical frameworks such as Markov Random Fields and potential based representations for encoding dependence mechanisms. We also describe decision systems, including LLM-integrated agent architectures, as feedback-driven stochastic processes where state-dependent dynamics may induce emergent macroscopic behavior. This perspective highlights the importance of having adequate models for the data as a prerequi- site for expanding predictive capability, and situates algorithmic learning within the informational limits imposed by the models.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成式建模形式化为路径积分框架，其中基于流、扩散、变分和对抗的模型均为同一主作用量的不同评估原则。其MSRJD形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，并开放给图论微扰论处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式建模方法（流、扩散、变分、对抗）看似互不相关，缺乏统一的数学框架；同时确定性采样的误差缺乏系统性的微扰修正手段。

Method: 将生成模型表述为路径积分，利用MSRJD形式体系区分自由与相互作用概率流，引入图论微扰展开；推导出一圈修正用于确定性采样器；将不完美学习的分数视为插入项，推导出响应加权评分匹配目标；对称等变漂移设计表述为带有EFT幂次计数的算子展开。

Result: 所提出的一圈修正无需额外的随机采样成本，在可解和非线性漂移上验证有效，将53%的树级误差降低至1.6%。

Conclusion: 该统一框架不仅为多种生成范式提供统一的理论基础，还直接带来无需额外采样成本的微扰修正，并为评分匹配目标和对称性设计提供新的原则性推导。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [216] [When Can You Trust Offline Evaluation of Equal-Cost Top-k Allocation? A Controlled, Reproducible Benchmark and Practitioner's Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12489)
*Binshuang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对离线策略评估（OPE）中的确定性top-k策略进行了系统基准测试。作者在五个数据集、两次已知效应扫描中对比了六种估计器，并用非模拟配对参照验证机制。研究发现三个核心问题：弱重叠由logger与目标动作的对齐决定而非logger锐度；优化者诅咒无法通过交叉拟合结果扰动项修复，需要策略级分割（改变估计目标）；倾向性估计误差是最大的性能退化来源，其对IPS的伤害超过其他所有压力测试。研究还发现有效样本量（ESS）在跨环境排名风险有效，但在单一日志内排名候选中较弱且临界点不可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在预算约束下决定对待哪些对象，并在部署前需要知道目标分配规则能带来多少收益。虽然离线策略评估（OPE）承诺可以从日志数据中评估这一点，但可部署的规则是确定性top-k策略，它消除了所有对动作的平均，导致弱重叠问题直接冲击估计。因此需要系统性基准测试来理解不同估计器在这种场景下的行为。

Method: 在五个数据集上对六种估计器进行基准测试，包含两次已知效应扫描。将日志数据合成并设置倾向性得分下限为0.02，确保所有权重有界。使用非模拟配对参照来验证机制，并区分合成与半合成精确值表面。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 弱重叠由logger与目标动作的对齐决定——用目标自身得分构建的logger即使锐化也几乎不改变重叠，而动作级分歧会崩溃重叠；ESS排名环境风险有效但在单日志内较弱且临界点不可迁移。(2) 交叉拟合结果扰动项无法修复优化者诅咒，反而使重用偏差更严重；诚实的策略级分割通过改变估计目标来避免重用。(3) 倾向性估计误差是最大退化源：折外估计对IPS伤害最大，对DR影响极小，甚至可能反转重叠诊断本身。倾向性下限使两个调优混合估计器退化为其未调优父体。

Conclusion: 在确定性top-k策略的OPE中，倾向性估计误差是最严重的威胁；弱重叠的诊断和治理应关注logger与目标的动作级对齐而非logger锐度；优化者诅咒需要策略级分割（改变估计目标）而非简单的交叉拟合。基准测试已公开发布（仅含公共数据）。

Abstract: Organizations decide whom to treat under a budget and want to know what a targeting rule would have earned before deploying it. Off-policy evaluation promises this from logged data, but the deployable rule is a deterministic top-k policy: it removes all averaging over actions, so weak overlap hits the estimate directly. We benchmark six estimators across five datasets and two known-effect sweeps, and validate the mechanisms against a non-simulated paired reference. First, weak overlap is governed by logger-target action alignment, not by logging sharpness alone: what governs support is the logger's probability of the target's actions. Sharpening a logger built from the target's own score barely moves overlap over the tested range; action-level disagreement collapses it. Effective sample size ranks this risk across logging environments, but is weak at ranking candidates within the single log a practitioner holds, and its cut point does not transfer. Second, the optimizer's curse is not fixed by cross-fitting the outcome nuisance. When the rule is fit on the data used to evaluate it, cross-fitting the nuisance alone leaves the reuse bias in place and makes it worse. Honest policy-level splitting avoids the reuse by targeting the learning procedure's value -- a change of estimand, not a de-biasing of the full-sample policy. Third, propensity-estimation error is the largest degradation we measure: an out-of-fold estimate hurts IPS more than any other stress we apply, leaves doubly-robust estimation almost unchanged, and can invert the overlap diagnostic itself. Logging is synthesized and propensities floored at 0.02, so every failure occurs with bounded weights; the floor also reduces the two tuned hybrids to their untuned parents, leaving four practically distinct estimators, and all exact-value surfaces are synthetic or semi-synthetic. We release the benchmark; public data only.

</details>


### [217] [Interpretable Causal Discovery via Causal-Effect Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12640)
*Cixuan Zhang,Guy Van den Broeck,Benjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于罕见事件估计技术的贝叶斯条件因果发现方法，在因果图与参数的联合空间上执行推断，以条件于因果效应约束等事件的方式揭示因果结构，并通过粒子逐渐推向约束区域来近似条件后验分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现的目标不仅是预测因果边，还要能够解释和阐释观察到的或假设的现象（如特别大的因果效应）。现有贝叶斯因果发现方法在面对后验质量较小的事件时计算困难，因此需要一种能够在联合图-参数空间中处理条件推断的新方法。

Method: 将条件因果发现建模为贝叶斯推断问题，目标是在给定事件（如因果效应约束）的条件下推断因果图与参数的后验分布。为应对小后验质量事件带来的计算挑战，借鉴罕见事件估计技术，将粒子群逐步向约束区域驱动，同时维持近似条件后验的样本。

Result: 在合成图上进行了小规模和大规模实证评估，验证了方法的准确性；并在Sachs蛋白数据集上的案例研究中展示了该方法可通过通路级摘要辅助科学探索。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效处理条件因果发现中的小后验质量事件，为科学探索提供通路级解释，在合成数据上验证了不同规模下的准确性和实用性。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover the underlying causal relationships given data generated from a system. The goal, however, is not merely to predict causal edges given data, but also to be able to interpret and explain either observed or hypothesized phenomena, such as a particularly large causal effect. We consider this task of conditional causal discovery and cast it as a Bayesian inference problem, in which we target the posterior over causal graphs and parameters conditional on an event such as a causal-effect constraint. Unfortunately, this poses a computational challenge: existing approaches to Bayesian causal discovery struggle when the event has small posterior mass. To address this, we adapt rare-event estimation techniques to perform inference the joint graph-parameter space. Our method gradually drives a particle population toward the constrained region while maintaining samples that approximate the conditional posterior. Empirical evaluation on synthetic graphs validates the accuracy of our approach at small and large scales, and we show in a case study on the Sachs protein dataset how our method can be used to aid scientific exploration by providing pathway-level summaries.

</details>


### [218] [Training Under Challenge: Executable Certificates and Challenge-Closed Optimality for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12655)
*Farhang Yeganegi,Arian Eamaz,Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Training Under Challenge"框架，一种可执行证书机制，用于诊断神经网络训练中的平坦损失曲线，通过架构有效的挑战程序构造备选解来量化全局最优性差距，并提供覆盖性与残差界的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦的训练曲线无法区分网络是否达到全局最优、陷入局部陷阱、受表示能力限制或与训练器不匹配。作者希望提供一种可执行、可验证的框架来系统诊断这些问题，而不仅仅是启发式判断。

Method: 该框架要求预先声明架构有效的流程，构造同一认证类别中的完整备选方案并重新评估相同目标函数。任何产生更低值的候选者都可作为可重放的见证，界定检查点的经验全局最优性差距。通过有限的挑战套件只能得到相对结论，全局差距需要单独的覆盖机制。作者定义了资源索引的挑战能力模数，在平方损失下基于块下降算子使覆盖性可检验，并得出均匀残余界与实际残余界。

Result: 作者证明了在没有覆盖性保证的情况下，一阶ReLU训练器可达到无穷多个精确条件头最优值同时收敛到非全局点（逆向前沿）。在通道门控ResNet-18蒸馏任务中，8个内部挑战覆盖了全部240个审计输出方向，实际残余界在真实差距的1.74-3.02倍范围内。配对预测证书能区分解码器利用不足与表示不足，量化去噪实验展示了诊断、修复和重新认证能力。

Conclusion: Training Under Challenge提供了一个可执行的证书框架，通过覆盖性驱动的挑战机制对神经网络的全局最优性差距进行量化诊断，能够区分不同类型的训练失败模式，并支持修复后的重新认证。

Abstract: A flat training curve does not reveal whether a neural network has reached a global optimum, is locally trapped, is representation-limited, or is mismatched to its trainer. We introduce Training Under Challenge, an executable-certificate framework in which predeclared, architecture-valid procedures construct complete alternatives in the same certified class and reevaluate the same objective. Any lower-valued candidate is a replayable witness that lower-bounds the checkpoint's empirical global-optimality gap. Passing a finite suite is only suite-relative; global-gap conclusions require a separately justified coverage mechanism. We define a resource-indexed challenge-power modulus that characterizes the largest gap compatible with passage. For squared loss, current block-decrease operators make coverage checkable and yield uniform and realized-residual bounds. We prove the converse frontier: without coverage, a first-order ReLU trainer can reach infinitely many exact conditional head optima while converging to a non-global point. On a channel-gated ResNet-18 distillation problem with known optimum, eight internal challenges cover all 240 audited output directions, and realized-residual bounds lie within factors of 1.74--3.02 of the true gap. Paired predictive certificates separate decoder under-use from representation insufficiency, while quantized-denoising studies demonstrate diagnosis, repair, and current-state recertification.

</details>


### [219] [CardioState-JEPA: Delay-Aware Cross-Modal Learning of a Shared Cardiac Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12944)
*Hamza Shafiq,Hung Manh Pham,Bin Zhu,Pan Zhou,Jun Hu,Aaqib Saeed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CardioState-JEPA，一个基于生理感知联合嵌入预测架构的心脏基础模型，在ECG、PPG、PCG三种模态上学习统一的共享表示，通过预测掩蔽的潜在心脏状态而非传感器特定的波形外观来实现跨模态互监督。在25个下游任务中以冻结编码器评估，PPG分类提升8.2 AUROC点、PCG杂音检测提升18.8 AUROC点、ECG分类提升15.5 AUROC点，并超越若干使用临床文本或监督标签训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG、PPG和PCG是同一心动周期的互补视图，但现有心脏基础模型仅针对单一传感模态训练，未利用跨传感器的共享生理学信息，导致模态间的共性知识被浪费。

Method: CardioState-JEPA采用生理感知的联合嵌入预测架构：将异构波形映射到公共token空间，用单一共享Transformer编码器处理，通过预测掩蔽的潜在心脏状态进行学习。引入学习的延迟对齐器来处理电学、机械和血流动力学事件间的时间偏移，将跨模态预测在相应的心脏时间上匹配。训练策略为先从丰富的单模态数据中学习模态内结构，再利用配对数据在潜在心脏时间上对齐模态。

Result: 在25个下游任务（覆盖ECG、PPG、PCG）中以冻结编码器评估，相较最佳自监督信号基线：PPG分类平均提升8.2 AUROC点，PCG杂音检测提升18.8 AUROC点，ECG分类提升15.5 AUROC点；在多个ECG基准上匹配或超越使用特权临床文本或监督标签训练的心脏模型。

Conclusion: 异构心脏信号能够相互监督，共同驱动一个统一的心脏生理学基础模型，证明了跨模态自我监督在心脏表征学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG), photoplethysmography (PPG), and phonocardiography (PCG) provide complementary views of the same cardiac cycle, yet existing cardiac foundation models are trained for a single sensing modality, leaving the shared physiology across sensors unexploited. We introduce CardioState-JEPA, a cardiac foundation model to learn a single shared representation jointly across ECG, PPG, and PCG, built on a physiology-aware joint-embedding predictive architecture. The model maps heterogeneous waveforms into a common token space, processes them with a single shared Transformer encoder, and learns by predicting masked latent cardiac states, placing the pretraining target on shared physiology rather than sensor-specific waveform appearance. To handle the temporal offsets between electrical, mechanical, and hemodynamic events, cross-modal prediction uses a learned delay aligner that matches signals at the corresponding cardiac time. Because synchronized multi-sensor recordings are scarce, CardioState-JEPA first learns within-modality structure from abundant unimodal data and then uses paired data to align modalities in latent cardiac time. Evaluated as a frozen encoder across 25 downstream tasks spanning ECG, PPG, and PCG, our encoder improves average PPG classification by 8.2 AUROC points, PCG murmur detection by 18.8 AUROC points, and ECG classification by 15.5 AUROC points over the best self-supervised signal baseline and matches or exceeds cardiac models trained with privileged clinical text or supervised labels on several ECG benchmarks. These results establish that heterogeneous cardiac signals can mutually supervise a single foundation model of cardiac physiology.

</details>


### [220] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoKiFormer 是一种新型 LLM 架构，通过引入局部融合注意力（LFA）和知识记忆模块（KMM）分别解决自注意力缺乏局部归纳偏置和 MoE 将知识存储与计算路径耦合的问题，预训练收敛速度比基线快 1.33 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 架构在预训练阶段存在两大低效问题：(i) 自注意力缺乏对局部信息的显式归纳偏置，导致序列内部局部信息被冗余建模；(ii) MoE 将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合，阻碍了对序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。

Method: 在标准 decoder 上增加两个专用模块：1) 局部融合注意力（LFA），将卷积融合引入注意力机制，显式捕捉局部模式，使注意力在更具信息量的表示上运行；2) 知识记忆模块（KMM），引入参数化键值记忆，将全局知识显式存储在可寻址的槽位中，实现存储与计算的解耦，支持直接知识检索。

Result: LoKiFormer 在预训练中比基线模型收敛速度快 1.33 倍，验证了其在信息集成效率和有效性上的优越性。

Conclusion: LoKiFormer 通过显式解耦局部模式捕捉（LFA）与全局知识存储（KMM），在局部与全局两个层面实现了更高效的信息融合，优于现有 LLM 架构。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [221] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散采样的掩码扩散（masking diffusion）方法，提出了一种新的数据几何度量——"去掩码增长复杂度"（UGC），其局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差。基于此度量，作者设计了优化的单块和多块调度策略，并证明了可以实现认证最优的采样器，其迭代复杂度在常数因子内接近理论最优。在细分区极限下，UGC密度的平方根积分决定了最优欧拉离散化误差的首阶项。实验表明，相对于粗略调度可实现~Ω(√d)的维度相关改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在生成建模中具有重要应用，但离散采样中的调度策略（scheduling）通常依赖启发式方法，缺乏对数据几何结构的理论理解和自适应性。现有分析未能统一不同掩码方案（如Bernoulli子集与固定基数），也未能量化自适应计算努力带来的收益，因此需要一种路径级别的度量来精确描述数据几何与KL离散化误差之间的关系。

Method: 本文引入路径解析的"去掩码增长复杂度"（UGC）度量，其局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差。在log-reveal-odds坐标下，作者推导出优化的单块和多块调度方案，并证明UGC增量可通过耦合揭示轨迹上的KL增量从样本中估计，从而构建认证最优采样器。通过折叠UGC路径得到聚合质量，与经典多变量依赖度量及先前离散扩散分析中的复杂度度量建立联系，并在细分区极限下推导出最优欧拉误差的首阶常数。

Result: 作者构建了统一分析框架，涵盖Bernoulli子集和固定基数去掩码方案；提出了可从样本估计UGC增量的方法，实现了以高概率达到指定KL误差且迭代复杂度在常数因子内接近oracle过程的最优采样器；在细分区极限下确定了最优欧拉离散化误差的锐利首阶项；实验示例展示了维度相关的显著增益，包括使用常数个自适应放置块可在调度上实现~Ω(√d)的改进。

Conclusion: UGC为离散掩码扩散采样提供了一种新的、可估计的、认证最优的调度设计框架，将数据几何、KL误差与采样复杂度统一在单一路径层面度量下，并在理论上确立了其与经典依赖度和先前复杂度度量的联系，为离散扩散模型的自适应高效采样奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [222] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于免费的卫星数据（雷达干涉测量与卫星天气），针对尼泊尔喜马拉雅地区的冰湖溃决、降雨触发滑坡和冰川小洪水三类灾害，提出了"哪个地点易感"与"何时触发"的预测模型。使用589次冰湖溃决事件和数千次滑坡事件，在空间交叉验证下与相似未溃决场地配对评估。结果显示：前期天气对三类灾害的触发时间预测ROC值分别为0.73、0.83和0.82；地形对易感性的排序能力在匹配对照后诚实值仅为0.76、0.71和0.54（后者不优于随机）。冰湖溃决信号在尼泊尔单地区达到0.89。五种深度学习模型未能决定性超越梯度提升基线，对冰湖灾害基线甚至胜出并可由三规则决策树复现。论文最后给出尼泊尔优先关注清单，作为排序辅助而非预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前可行性研究仅发现形变可指示"哪个湖在失稳"、天气可指示"何时有风险"，但未提出可操作的预测模型。本文旨在弥补这一空白，基于免费数据构建并评估能够预测"哪个地点易感、何时触发"的模型，并检验其是否优于强基线。

Method: 使用HMAGLOFDB中589次有日期标注的溃决事件及数千次滑坡事件，将每个事件与相似但未溃决的场地匹配；模型接受空间交叉验证（按整幅地图瓦片 withheld），防止因识别训练过的邻近区域而虚高。采用前期天气特征预测触发时机，地形特征评估易感性，并将五种深度学习模型与梯度提升基线及三规则决策树对比。

Result: 前期天气对三类灾害（大溃决、滑坡、小洪水）的ROC分别为0.73、0.83、0.82；地形易感性在朴素评分下接近0.9但匹配对照后为0.76、0.71和0.54（后者无增益）；冰湖溃决信号在尼泊尔单一地区达0.89。深度学习模型未决定性胜出：仅滑坡上略高三分（不足以确认），冰湖灾害基线胜出且可被三规则决策树（崎岖度+季风雨）复现。

Conclusion: 免费卫星数据足以支撑有意义的灾害风险排序与触发时间预测，但地形对易感性的区分能力有限，且复杂模型并不优于简单基线。论文以尼泊尔优先关注清单作为排序辅助工具收尾，并明确标识免费数据的应用边界。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [223] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多标签分类与二值分割中Jaccard得分（IoU）的校准与可代理性。证明了Jaccard得分矩阵的非奇异性和仿射维度性质，给出了校准维度的上下界（2^{s-1} ≤ CCdim ≤ 2^s-1），并提出了两种多项式维度的近似代理（F1-to-Jaccard转移与MinHash平方损失代理），表明零遗憾校准需要指数维度，而固定加性遗憾容差则只需多项式维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jaccard得分是多标签分类和二值分割中的标准评估指标，但其在损失校准（calibration）框架下的理论性质——特别是精确校准所需的预测维度——尚不清楚。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统刻画Jaccard损失的校准维度，并探索是否存在低维的替代代理函数。

Method: 采用三重技术路线：(1) 利用有限MinHash Gram表示结合布尔Möbius反演证明Jaccard、shifted-loss及ordinary loss矩阵的非奇异性与仿射维度性质；(2) 通过构造因子加权分布（含2^{s-1}+1个支撑点）和Bayes最优报告，建立校准维度的下界；(3) 构造两种近似代理：一个基于已有的(s^2+1)维F1代理的F1-to-Jaccard遗憾转移，以及一个基于MinHash平方损失（含直接构造与有符号变体）的代理，并给出显式的遗憾转移界。

Result: (1) 证明Jaccard矩阵非奇异，损失列仿射维度为2^s-1；(2) 校准维度满足2^{s-1} ≤ CCdim(L^Jac) ≤ 2^s-1，因此精确校准的凸代理需要指数多个预测坐标；(3) F1-to-Jaccard转移得到渐近Jaccard遗憾上界3-2√2；(4) MinHash平方损失代理在任意条件标签分布上均匀地达到α遗憾，直接构造维度为O((s^2+s log(1/ρ))/α^2)，有符号变体维度为O((s+log(1/ρ))/α^2)。

Conclusion: 零遗憾的精确校准必然要求指数维度的预测空间，这一结论基于校准维度的下界；然而在实际应用中，任何固定的加性遗憾容差α都可通过多项式维度的代理实现，这表明在理论精确性与实践效率之间存在明确的分界。MinHash平方损失代理为实际多标签分类提供了可操作的近似Jaccard优化方案。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [224] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MARCH（Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History），一种混合架构，通过周期性缓存循环状态检查点作为状态锚点，并引入内容条件锚键实现锚点级注意力路由，在保持线性计算效率的同时将循环状态模型（SSM）的记忆容量扩展到超越固定尺寸维度，在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上均优于多种线性注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer虽具备随上下文长度增长的分词级记忆，但训练时具有二次方计算复杂度且推理时KV缓存线性增长；循环替代方案（如SSM）虽然解码高效，但将全部历史压缩为固定尺寸状态，早期关联容易被后续更新覆盖，导致在召回密集型任务上表现不佳。如何在保持高效解码的同时，增强状态空间模型的长序列记忆容量是核心问题。

Method: MARCH的核心装置包括：(1) 状态锚点（state anchors）：周期性缓存累积的循环状态检查点；(2) 锚键（anchor keys）：为每个锚点生成紧凑的内容条件化键；(3) 锚点查询（anchor query）：每个token生成锚点查询，对所有因果可用的历史状态锚点进行注意力聚合；(4) 记忆库（memory bank）：随上下文长度增长，提供历史分辨率与存储成本之间的可控权衡。输出为对所有历史锚点沿当前状态进行注意力风格聚合的结果。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文内检索任务上均一致优于多种线性注意力变体，证明了内容路由的状态缓存能显著增强循环长程记忆，同时保留其原生计算路径的效率。

Conclusion: MARCH证明了通过内容条件化路由（content-routed）的状态缓存机制，可以有效弥合Transformer式可扩展记忆与循环模型高效解码之间的差距，为长上下文建模提供了一条兼顾性能与效率的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state.
